## ATLAS Align Update Request Template

Use this template for feature work, bug fixes, UI adjustments, refactors, performance changes, database or API work, imports or exports, audit-rule changes, and documentation updates.

Complete every relevant section. Use `Not applicable` when a section does not apply. Do not delete safeguards merely to shorten a request. For a narrow, low-risk update, keep the same headings but answer them concisely.

<!-- ATLAS-APP-VERSION:PROJECT-NOTE:BEGIN -->
**Project version note:** The current ATLAS Align application version is `v3.0.2`
<!-- ATLAS-APP-VERSION:PROJECT-NOTE:END -->

---

# 1. Title

**Request type:**

- [ ] New feature
- [ ] Bug fix
- [ ] UI or CSS adjustment
- [ ] Performance improvement
- [ ] Database or API change
- [ ] Refactor
- [ ] Export or import change
- [ ] Audit logic or business-rule change
- [ ] Documentation only
- [ ] Other:

**Title:** [Short, specific outcome-oriented title]

**Risk classification:**

- [ ] Low: localized presentation or behavior change with no persisted-data, API, authorization, or business-rule impact
- [ ] Medium: state, workflow, import/export, or cross-module behavior change with bounded impact
- [ ] High: database, migration, authorization, security, destructive operation, source-of-truth, or material audit-rule change

---

# 2. Implementation Contract

This section is the controlling summary. Detailed sections below may clarify it but must not contradict it.

**Goal:**  
[One or two sentences describing the required outcome.]

**Expected files allowed to change:**

- [List only files that current evidence indicates are likely to require modification.]

**Required generated artifacts:**

- [List generated files that must be rebuilt and delivered, or state `None`.]

**Conditional files:**

- [Example: `css/manifest.json` only if changed by the CSS build.]
- [Example: `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` only if a documented maintenance trigger occurs.]

**Behavior that must remain unchanged:**

- [List the most important protected workflows, contracts, and business rules.]

**Definition of done:**

- [Summarize the essential observable results.]
- [Summarize the minimum required validation.]
- [State the requested deliverable format.]

**Blocking assumptions or decisions:**  
[List only matters that could cause an incorrect business rule, unsafe migration, security issue, wrong source of truth, irreversible change, or major regression. If none, state `No known blocking assumptions or decisions.`]

**Implementation flexibility:**  
The desired behavior and acceptance criteria are mandatory. Any implementation approach described in this request is a preference unless explicitly labeled `Required implementation`. If current source evidence shows that a preferred approach is unsafe or incorrect, use the smallest safe alternative and explain the deviation.

- [ ] Intermittently

- [ ] Unknown

**Business or technical impact:**  
[Explain the consequence without overstating unverified risk.]

- [ ] Yes
- [ ] No

# 4. Verified Source Evidence

Separate facts directly established by current authoritative materials from hypotheses that still require validation.

## 4.1 Source Authority

Use sources in this order unless the request explicitly states otherwise:

1. Most recently attached current files and current-request instructions
2. `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`
3. `PROJECT_TREE.txt`
4. Current screenshots, logs, console output, recordings, and Inspect Element details
5. Explicitly identified enterprise knowledge source
6. Related current source files and established project patterns
7. Earlier conversation context
8. Historical patches or archived versions only when explicitly requested

**Authoritative materials attached:**

- [List each current file, screenshot, log, or sample.]

**Important version instruction:**  
Use only the most recently attached current files as implementation inputs. Do not substitute archived files, prior-conversation copies, generated aggregates, repository-history versions, or enterprise copies unless explicitly directed.

## 4.2 Verified Facts

List only facts directly supported by the attached source, runtime evidence, or documentation.

- **Evidence:** [Exact observed behavior, declaration, function call, route, schema, error, or computed value.]  
  **Source:** [File and function/selector/line area, screenshot, log entry, or command output.]
- **Evidence:**  
  **Source:**

## 4.3 Implementation Hypotheses

List likely causes or solution directions that still require confirmation. Do not present them as verified facts.

- **Hypothesis:** [Likely root cause.]  
  **Why it is plausible:** [Evidence that supports but does not prove it.]  
  **How to validate:** [Focused code inspection, computed-style check, runtime state check, test, or command.]

If the root cause is fully proven by current evidence, state why it is verified and omit speculative alternatives.

## 4.4 Unknowns

- [List relevant unknowns.]
- [Mark each as blocking or non-blocking.]

For a non-blocking unknown, follow existing patterns, make the smallest reasonable assumption, and disclose it in the delivery summary.

# 6. Acceptance Criteria

Keep this list concise and non-duplicative. Each criterion should describe one independently observable result. Combine closely related presentation details into one criterion and place procedural checks in the Validation Plan instead.

Recommended target:

- Narrow or low-risk update: 5 to 10 criteria
- Medium-risk update: 8 to 15 criteria
- High-risk update: only as many as needed to cover distinct business, security, migration, and recovery outcomes

1. [Primary user-visible result.]
2. [State or data result.]
3. [Boundary or error condition.]
4. [Key preserved behavior.]
5. [Build or deployable-artifact requirement, if relevant.]
6. [Accessibility or responsive result, if relevant.]
7. [No unintended persistence, API, database, or authorization change, if relevant.]

Do not create separate criteria that merely restate the same resolved style, value, or state on multiple elements unless those elements can fail independently.

---

These categories are strict.

## 7.1 Files Allowed to Change

Only files in this list may be modified without first demonstrating a verified technical requirement.

- `[path]`  
  **Expected responsibility in this update:** [Why modification may be necessary.]

## 7.2 Required Generated Artifacts

Generated files may be included only when they are required deployable outputs of modified source files.

- `[path]`  
  **Generation command/source:** [How it is produced.]

Do not edit generated artifacts manually unless the project explicitly defines them as editable.

## 7.3 Review-only Files

These files may be inspected for dependencies, contracts, cascade, load order, documentation, or validation, but must not be changed unless new evidence proves that a change is technically required.

- `[path]`  
  **Reason for review:**

If a review-only file must change:

1. State the verified reason before expanding scope.
2. Explain why the change cannot safely remain in an allowed file.
3. Add the file to the modified-file list in the delivery summary.

## 7.4 Protected Files and Areas

These files or areas must not change as part of this request unless the request is revised because of a blocking technical requirement.

- `[path or area]`
- APIs: [protected routes/contracts]
- Database: [protected tables, migrations, or state]
- Authentication/authorization: [protected behavior]
- Dependencies/toolchain: [protected behavior]

# 8. Technical Constraints

- Preserve existing architecture, folder structure, naming conventions, coding style, contracts, and deployment model.
- Make the smallest safe change that satisfies the desired behavior and acceptance criteria.
- Do not introduce unrelated refactoring, modernization, optimization, framework conversion, or dependency changes.
- Reuse existing utilities, renderers, state, services, routes, and patterns where safe.
- Preserve public signatures, IDs, data attributes, DOM contracts, API contracts, database contracts, accessibility, and script load order unless a change is explicitly required.
- Do not add global state when existing state or local scope is sufficient.
- Do not use placeholders such as `existing code unchanged` in replacement files.
- Do not invent file contents, functions, selectors, routes, data, behavior, or test results.
- Do not change a review-only or protected file merely to match an expected package tree.
- Do not expose or package secrets, tokens, private keys, production connection strings, or populated `.env` files.
- Never claim that a command, test, browser check, deployment, or measurement passed unless it was actually performed.

**Project-specific constraints:**

- [Classic script load-order requirements.]
- [CSS source/build requirements.]
- [Database transaction/migration constraints.]
- [HRIS audit business-rule constraints.]
- [Import/export format constraints.]
- [Performance constraints only if performance is in scope.]

# 10. Validation Plan

Request only validation that is relevant and realistically executable with the available files and environment.

## 10.1 Required Before Delivery

Select focused checks based on changed files and risk:

- [ ] Syntax or type validation for modified code
- [ ] Relevant build or generated-artifact check
- [ ] Existing targeted automated tests
- [ ] File-path, import, reference, and load-order review
- [ ] Scoped diff and whitespace check
- [ ] Package integrity and package-content check
- [ ] Focused static contract review

**Exact commands:**

```bash
# Add only commands supported by the current repository.
```

## 10.2 Focused Runtime or Manual Validation

List the smallest set of runtime checks that proves the acceptance criteria and highest-risk regressions.

1. [Primary success path.]
2. [Most important state transition or boundary case.]
3. [Highest-risk preserved workflow.]
4. [Responsive/accessibility check if the UI changed.]

## 10.3 Conditional Validation

Run only if the implementation touches the corresponding area:

- Database or migration validation
- API request/response inspection
- Import/export sample validation
- Browser computed-style inspection
- Performance measurement against an explicit baseline
- Deployment smoke test
- Accessibility tooling

## 10.4 Evidence Rule

For every reported validation:

- Provide the exact command or browser action.
- Provide the actual result.
- Use `Passed` or `Verified` only when the check was performed successfully.
- Use `Failed` when it was performed and failed.
- Use `Not performed` when the environment or supplied files did not permit the check.
- Provide the exact follow-up command or manual step for any important check not performed.
- Do not infer browser, API, database, deployment, performance, or accessibility results from static source review alone.

---

Review `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` against its maintenance triggers.

Update `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` only if the implementation changes one or more of the following:

- Application version or technology stack
- Directory structure or documented file responsibility
- Database schema or migration sequence
- New, removed, or renamed JavaScript functions
- Current priorities or known technical debt
- Coding standards or architectural constraints

**Packaging rule:**  
If a maintenance trigger occurs, update the complete `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` and include it in the deliverable. Do not update or package `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` merely because code changed.

# 12. Deliverable

**Requested format:**

- [ ] Full replacement files in a ZIP package
- [ ] Modified files only in a ZIP package
- [ ] Single patch file
- [ ] Analysis and implementation plan only
- [ ] Documentation update only
- [ ] Other:

**Preferred package name:**  
`[descriptive-package-name].zip`

## 12.1 Actual-change Packaging Rule

The initial expected file list is a planning aid, not a required package tree.

Include only:

- Files actually modified to implement the accepted behavior
- Required generated artifacts produced from those modifications
- `css/manifest.json` or equivalent only if the build actually changes it
- `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` only if a documented maintenance trigger occurs
- Migration files only when a database change is explicitly in scope and implemented

Exclude:

- Unchanged review files
- Aggregate review text files
- Screenshots, logs, and temporary files
- Historical patches and prior packages
- `node_modules`
- Secrets, credentials, tokens, private keys, production connection strings, or populated `.env` files
- Files changed only to make the package resemble the forecast

Preserve project-relative paths. Do not flatten directories.

**Illustrative expected package, if the preferred approach is sufficient:**

```text
[package-name].zip
├── [modified source]
└── [required generated artifact]
```

The final package may differ from this illustration when actual verified changes differ. Explain every difference.

---

# 13. Evidence-based Delivery Summary

Keep the delivery summary concise and use these headings:

- **Root cause:** Verified cause, or remaining hypothesis if runtime confirmation was not possible
- **Implementation:** What changed and why
- **Modified source files:** Actual modified source files only
- **Generated artifacts:** Actual regenerated files only
- **Conditional files:** Whether manifest, migration, lockfile, or `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` changed and why
- **Validation performed:** Exact commands/actions and actual results
- **Validation not performed:** Important checks not completed and exact follow-up steps
- **Assumptions:** Non-blocking assumptions used
- **Known risks or limitations:** Remaining uncertainty or environment limitation
- **Scope confirmation:** Explicit confirmation of protected areas not changed
- **Application steps:** Exact extraction, build, migration, or deployment steps that apply
- **Verification steps:** Focused user/runtime checks
- **Download:** Link to the requested artifact

Do not repeat the entire feature request in the delivery summary.

---

# 14. Detailed Reference Appendix, if Needed

Use this appendix only when detail would distract from the controlling implementation contract. Omit subsections that are not relevant.

## 14.1 Current-version Fingerprint

Capture a repository revision and working-tree status whenever Git metadata is available:

```bash
printf '===== REPOSITORY FINGERPRINT =====\n'
git rev-parse HEAD
git status --short
```

Capture hashes for authoritative implementation inputs:

```bash
printf '\n===== AUTHORITATIVE FILE HASHES =====\n'
sha256sum \
  path/to/file-one \
  path/to/file-two \
  PROJECT_CONTEXT.md
```

For a combined review bundle, place the fingerprint before file contents:

```bash
{
  printf '===== REPOSITORY FINGERPRINT =====\n\n'
  if git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf 'Commit: '
    git rev-parse HEAD
    printf '\nWorking tree status:\n'
    git status --short
  else
    printf 'Git metadata unavailable\n'
  fi

  printf '\n===== AUTHORITATIVE FILE HASHES =====\n\n'
  sha256sum \
    path/to/file-one \
    path/to/file-two \
    PROJECT_CONTEXT.md

  for file in \
    path/to/file-one \
    path/to/file-two \
    PROJECT_CONTEXT.md
  do
    if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then
      printf 'ERROR: required file not found: %s\n' "$file" >&2
      exit 1
    fi
    printf '\n===== BEGIN FILE: %s =====\n\n' "$file"
    cat "$file"
    printf '\n\n===== END FILE: %s =====\n' "$file"
  done
} > implementation-review.txt
```

**Fingerprint rules:**

- Record the fingerprint when the request bundle is created.
- Do not modify a file solely because its hash appears in the request.
- In the delivery summary, state whether implementation used the supplied fingerprinted versions.
- If a replacement file no longer matches the supplied input revision because the user provided a newer authoritative version, use the newer version and disclose the change.
- Do not treat a dirty working tree as an automatic blocker, but identify unrelated existing changes before overwriting files.

## Final Request-quality Check

Before sending the request, confirm:

- [ ] The Implementation Contract is short and controlling.
- [ ] User-observable behavior is separated from diagnosis.
- [ ] Verified facts are separated from hypotheses and unknowns.
- [ ] Desired behavior is stated as outcomes rather than unnecessary code instructions.
- [ ] Acceptance criteria are concise and non-duplicative.
- [ ] Files allowed to change are distinct from review-only and protected files.
- [ ] Validation is focused by risk and executable in the available environment.
- [ ] The request includes a repository commit or file hashes when possible.
- [ ] Documentation triggers were evaluated against `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`.
- [ ] Deliverable contents depend on actual changes, not the forecast.
- [ ] The requested delivery summary is concise and evidence-based.

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: build-css.mjs
================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const currentFile = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const root = path.dirname(currentFile);
const cssRoot = path.join(root, "css");
const manifestPath = path.join(cssRoot, "manifest.json");
const args = new Set(process.argv.slice(2));
const checkOnly = args.has("--check");
const noDiscover = args.has("--no-discover");
const acceptDeletions = args.has("--accept-deletions");
const knownArgs = new Set(["--check", "--no-discover", "--accept-deletions"]);
for (const arg of args)
  if (!knownArgs.has(arg)) throw new Error(`Unknown option: ${arg}`);

const BEGIN_RE = /\/\*\s*ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:(u\d+):BEGIN\s*\*\//g;
const END_RE = /\/\*\s*ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:(u\d+):END\s*\*\//g;
const INSERT_RE =
  /\/\*\s*ATLAS-INSERT-(AFTER|BEFORE):(u\d+)\s*\*\/|\/\*\s*ATLAS-INSERT:END\s*\*\//i;

function readJson(file) {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
}

function safeProjectPath(relativeFile) {
  if (
    typeof relativeFile !== "string" ||
    !relativeFile.startsWith("css/") ||
    !relativeFile.endsWith(".css")
  ) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid CSS source path in manifest: ${relativeFile}`);
  }
  const absolute = path.resolve(root, relativeFile);
  const expectedRoot = `${path.resolve(cssRoot)}${path.sep}`;
  if (!absolute.startsWith(expectedRoot))
    throw new Error(`CSS source path escapes css/: ${relativeFile}`);
  return absolute;
}

function stripCommentsAndWhitespace(value) {
  return value.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "").trim();
}

function validateCssStructure(source, label) {
  let depth = 0;
  let quote = null;
  let comment = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i += 1) {
    if (comment) {
      if (source[i] === "*" && source[i + 1] === "/") {
        comment = false;
        i += 1;
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (quote) {
      if (source[i] === "\\") {
        i += 1;
        continue;
      }
      if (source[i] === quote) quote = null;
      continue;
    }
    if (source[i] === "/" && source[i + 1] === "*") {
      comment = true;
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (source[i] === '"' || source[i] === "'") {
      quote = source[i];
      continue;
    }
    if (source[i] === "{") depth += 1;
    if (source[i] === "}") {
      depth -= 1;
      if (depth < 0)
        throw new Error(`${label} has an unmatched closing brace.`);
    }
  }
  if (comment) throw new Error(`${label} has an unterminated comment.`);
  if (quote) throw new Error(`${label} has an unterminated string.`);
  if (depth !== 0) throw new Error(`${label} has unbalanced braces.`);
}

function splitTopLevelUnits(source, label) {
  const units = [];
  let start = 0;
  let depth = 0;
  let quote = null;
  let comment = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i += 1) {
    if (comment) {
      if (source[i] === "*" && source[i + 1] === "/") {
        comment = false;
        i += 1;
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (quote) {
      if (source[i] === "\\") {
        i += 1;
        continue;
      }
      if (source[i] === quote) quote = null;
      continue;
    }
    if (source[i] === "/" && source[i + 1] === "*") {
      comment = true;
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (source[i] === '"' || source[i] === "'") {
      quote = source[i];
      continue;
    }
    if (source[i] === "{") depth += 1;
    else if (source[i] === "}") {
      depth -= 1;
      if (depth < 0)
        throw new Error(`${label} has an unmatched closing brace.`);
      if (depth === 0) {
        let end = i + 1;
        while (end < source.length && /\s/.test(source[end])) end += 1;
        units.push({ start, end, text: source.slice(start, end) });
        start = end;
        i = end - 1;
      }
    } else if (source[i] === ";" && depth === 0) {
      let end = i + 1;
      while (end < source.length && /\s/.test(source[end])) end += 1;
      units.push({ start, end, text: source.slice(start, end) });
      start = end;
      i = end - 1;
    }
  }
  if (comment || quote || depth !== 0) validateCssStructure(source, label);
  if (start < source.length)
    units.push({ start, end: source.length, text: source.slice(start) });
  return units.filter((unit) => stripCommentsAndWhitespace(unit.text) !== "");
}

function parseSourceFile(relativeFile, source) {
  const tokens = [];
  const occupied = [];
  BEGIN_RE.lastIndex = 0;
  let match;
  while ((match = BEGIN_RE.exec(source))) {
    const id = match[1];
    END_RE.lastIndex = BEGIN_RE.lastIndex;
    const endMatch = END_RE.exec(source);
    if (!endMatch || endMatch[1] !== id)
      throw new Error(
        `Missing or mismatched ${id} end marker in ${relativeFile}.`,
      );
    const duplicateEnd = source
      .slice(BEGIN_RE.lastIndex, endMatch.index)
      .match(/ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:u\d+:END/);
    if (duplicateEnd)
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected source-unit end marker inside ${id} in ${relativeFile}.`,
      );
    tokens.push({
      type: "existing",
      id,
      file: relativeFile,
      start: match.index,
      end: END_RE.lastIndex,
      content: source.slice(BEGIN_RE.lastIndex, endMatch.index),
    });
    occupied.push([match.index, END_RE.lastIndex]);
    BEGIN_RE.lastIndex = END_RE.lastIndex;
  }

  END_RE.lastIndex = 0;
  while ((match = END_RE.exec(source))) {
    if (
      !occupied.some(
        ([start, end]) => match.index >= start && match.index < end,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(`Orphan ${match[1]} end marker in ${relativeFile}.`);
    }
  }

  let cursor = 0;
  const outside = [];
  for (const item of tokens) {
    if (cursor < item.start)
      outside.push({
        start: cursor,
        end: item.start,
        text: source.slice(cursor, item.start),
      });
    cursor = item.end;
  }
  if (cursor < source.length)
    outside.push({
      start: cursor,
      end: source.length,
      text: source.slice(cursor),
    });

  const newUnits = [];
  for (const segment of outside) {
    for (const unit of splitTopLevelUnits(segment.text, relativeFile)) {
      const absoluteStart = segment.start + unit.start;
      const absoluteEnd = segment.start + unit.end;
      const before = source.slice(0, absoluteStart);
      const directiveMatches = [
        ...before.matchAll(new RegExp(INSERT_RE.source, "gi")),
      ];
      const lastDirective = directiveMatches.at(-1);
      let directive = null;
      if (
        lastDirective &&
        stripCommentsAndWhitespace(
          before.slice(lastDirective.index + lastDirective[0].length),
        ) === ""
      ) {
        directive = lastDirective[0].includes("ATLAS-INSERT:END")
          ? { type: "end" }
          : { type: lastDirective[1].toLowerCase(), id: lastDirective[2] };
      }
      newUnits.push({
        type: "new",
        file: relativeFile,
        start: absoluteStart,
        end: absoluteEnd,
        content: unit.text.replace(new RegExp(INSERT_RE.source, "gi"), ""),
        directive,
      });
    }
  }
  return { relativeFile, source, existing: tokens, newUnits };
}

function nextUnitNumber(manifest) {
  const numbers = manifest.units
    .map(({ id }) => Number.parseInt(String(id).slice(1), 10))
    .filter(Number.isFinite);
  return Math.max(0, ...numbers, Number(manifest.nextUnitNumber || 1) - 1) + 1;
}

function insertManifestUnit(units, entry, placement) {
  if (placement.type === "end") {
    units.push(entry);
    return;
  }
  const index = units.findIndex(({ id }) => id === placement.id);
  if (index < 0)
    throw new Error(`Insertion anchor ${placement.id} does not exist.`);
  units.splice(placement.type === "before" ? index : index + 1, 0, entry);
}

function inferPlacement(parsed, newUnit, manifest) {
  if (newUnit.directive) return newUnit.directive;
  const previous = parsed.existing
    .filter((item) => item.end <= newUnit.start)
    .at(-1);
  if (previous) return { type: "after", id: previous.id };
  const next = parsed.existing.find((item) => item.start >= newUnit.end);
  if (next) return { type: "before", id: next.id };
  const configured = manifest.files?.[parsed.relativeFile]?.defaultInsert;
  if (configured === "end") return { type: "end" };
  const configuredMatch = /^(before|after):(u\d+)$/i.exec(configured || "");
  if (configuredMatch)
    return { type: configuredMatch[1].toLowerCase(), id: configuredMatch[2] };
  throw new Error(
    `Cannot place new CSS in ${parsed.relativeFile}. Add /* ATLAS-INSERT-AFTER:u#### */, /* ATLAS-INSERT-BEFORE:u#### */, or configure manifest.files["${parsed.relativeFile}"].defaultInsert.`,
  );
}

function atomicWrite(file, content) {
  const temporary = `${file}.tmp-${process.pid}`;
  fs.writeFileSync(temporary, content, "utf8");
  fs.renameSync(temporary, file);
}

const manifest = readJson(manifestPath);
if (!Array.isArray(manifest.units))
  throw new Error("manifest.units must be an array.");
const manifestIds = new Set();
for (const unit of manifest.units) {
  if (!/^u\d+$/.test(unit.id)) throw new Error(`Invalid unit id: ${unit.id}`);
  if (manifestIds.has(unit.id))
    throw new Error(`Duplicate manifest unit id: ${unit.id}`);
  manifestIds.add(unit.id);
  safeProjectPath(unit.file);
}

const sourceFiles = new Set(manifest.units.map(({ file }) => file));
if (manifest.files)
  for (const file of Object.keys(manifest.files)) sourceFiles.add(file);
for (const name of fs.readdirSync(cssRoot))
  if (name.endsWith(".css")) sourceFiles.add(`css/${name}`);

const parsedFiles = new Map();
const foundUnits = new Map();
for (const relativeFile of [...sourceFiles].sort()) {
  const absolute = safeProjectPath(relativeFile);
  if (!fs.existsSync(absolute))
    throw new Error(`Missing CSS source file: ${relativeFile}`);
  const parsed = parseSourceFile(
    relativeFile,
    fs.readFileSync(absolute, "utf8"),
  );
  parsedFiles.set(relativeFile, parsed);
  for (const item of parsed.existing) {
    if (foundUnits.has(item.id))
      throw new Error(
        `Duplicate source unit ${item.id} in ${foundUnits.get(item.id).file} and ${relativeFile}.`,
      );
    foundUnits.set(item.id, item);
  }
}

const missing = manifest.units.filter(({ id }) => !foundUnits.has(id));
if (missing.length && !acceptDeletions) {
  throw new Error(
    `Manifest units missing from source files: ${missing.map(({ id }) => id).join(", ")}. Re-run with --accept-deletions to confirm removal.`,
  );
}
if (missing.length)
  manifest.units = manifest.units.filter(({ id }) => foundUnits.has(id));

for (const unit of manifest.units) {
  const found = foundUnits.get(unit.id);
  if (found && unit.file !== found.file) unit.file = found.file;
}

const discovered = [];
let nextNumber = nextUnitNumber(manifest);
for (const parsed of parsedFiles.values()) {
  const orderedNew = [...parsed.newUnits].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  for (const newUnit of orderedNew) {
    if (noDiscover)
      throw new Error(
        `Unmarked CSS detected in ${parsed.relativeFile}. Remove --no-discover or add source-unit markers.`,
      );
    const id = `u${String(nextNumber).padStart(4, "0")}`;
    nextNumber += 1;
    const placement = inferPlacement(parsed, newUnit, manifest);
    insertManifestUnit(
      manifest.units,
      { id, file: parsed.relativeFile },
      placement,
    );
    newUnit.id = id;
    discovered.push({ id, file: parsed.relativeFile, placement });
    parsed.existing.push({
      type: "existing",
      id,
      file: parsed.relativeFile,
      start: newUnit.start,
      end: newUnit.end,
      content: newUnit.content,
    });
  }
}
manifest.schemaVersion = Math.max(2, Number(manifest.schemaVersion || 1));
manifest.nextUnitNumber = nextNumber;

const rewrittenSources = new Map();
for (const parsed of parsedFiles.values()) {
  if (!parsed.newUnits.length) continue;
  let rewritten = parsed.source;
  for (const unit of [...parsed.newUnits].sort((a, b) => b.start - a.start)) {
    const wrapped = `/* ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:${unit.id}:BEGIN */${unit.content}/* ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:${unit.id}:END */`;
    rewritten = `${rewritten.slice(0, unit.start)}${wrapped}${rewritten.slice(unit.end)}`;
  }
  rewrittenSources.set(parsed.relativeFile, rewritten);
}

const sourceCache = new Map();
for (const [relativeFile, rewritten] of rewrittenSources)
  sourceCache.set(relativeFile, rewritten);
function sourceFor(relativeFile) {
  if (!sourceCache.has(relativeFile))
    sourceCache.set(
      relativeFile,
      fs.readFileSync(safeProjectPath(relativeFile), "utf8"),
    );
  return sourceCache.get(relativeFile);
}

const emitted = new Set();
const output = manifest.units
  .map(({ id, file }) => {
    if (emitted.has(id))
      throw new Error(`Unit ${id} would be emitted more than once.`);
    emitted.add(id);
    const source = sourceFor(file);
    const begin = `/* ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:${id}:BEGIN */`;
    const end = `/* ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:${id}:END */`;
    const start = source.indexOf(begin);
    if (start < 0) throw new Error(`Missing ${id} begin marker in ${file}.`);
    const contentStart = start + begin.length;
    const finish = source.indexOf(end, contentStart);
    if (finish < 0) throw new Error(`Missing ${id} end marker in ${file}.`);
    if (source.indexOf(begin, contentStart) >= 0)
      throw new Error(`Duplicate ${id} begin marker in ${file}.`);
    return source.slice(contentStart, finish);
  })
  .join("");

validateCssStructure(output, manifest.source || "styles.css");
if (/ATLAS-SOURCE-UNIT:/.test(output))
  throw new Error("Generated stylesheet contains source-management markers.");

if (checkOnly) {
  console.log(
    `CSS check passed. ${manifest.units.length} units would be emitted.`,
  );
  if (discovered.length) {
    console.log(`Discovered ${discovered.length} new CSS unit(s):`);
    for (const item of discovered)
      console.log(
        `  ${item.id}  ${item.file}  ${item.placement.type}${item.placement.id ? `:${item.placement.id}` : ""}`,
      );
  }
  if (missing.length)
    console.log(`Would remove ${missing.length} confirmed missing unit(s).`);
  process.exit(0);
}

for (const [relativeFile, contents] of rewrittenSources)
  atomicWrite(safeProjectPath(relativeFile), contents);
atomicWrite(manifestPath, `${JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2)}\n`);
atomicWrite(path.join(root, manifest.source || "styles.css"), output);
console.log(
  `Built ${manifest.source || "styles.css"} from ${manifest.units.length} ordered source units.`,
);
if (discovered.length)
  console.log(
    `Discovered, marked, and registered ${discovered.length} new CSS unit(s).`,
  );
if (missing.length)
  console.log(
    `Removed ${missing.length} confirmed missing unit(s) from the manifest.`,
  );


================================================================================
END FILE: build-css.mjs
================================================================================

/**
 * 01-state-dom.js
 *
 * Global state, cached DOM references, status/theme helpers, field-display helpers, and navigation helpers. Later modules read and mutate this shared state directly.
 * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
 */

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State initialization
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Creates a new empty audit object with the expected state shape.
 */
function defaultAudit() {
  return {
    // Generate a unique audit identifier using the shared uid helper (from 00-config-utils.js).
    id: uid("audit"),

    // Default display name used until the user provides an audit name.
    name: "Untitled Audit",

    // Optional audit purpose or business context entered by the user.
    purpose: "",

    // User-controlled audit workflow status.
    status: "Draft",

    // Source systems, files, tables, or datasets associated with this audit.
    sources: [],

    // Field definitions or mapped data elements used by the audit.
    fields: [],

    // User-controlled final output field selections. Logic usage remains derived.
    displayFields: [],

    // Audit workflow steps, validations, or review activities.
    steps: [],
  };
}

const state = {
  // Current working audit object loaded into the application.
  audit: defaultAudit(),

  // Tracks the currently selected audit step for detail editing or preview.
  selectedStepId: null,

  // Collection of saved audit flows available to the user.
  savedFlows: [],

  // Controls whether autosave is temporarily suspended.
  autosavePaused: false,

  // Cached repository/library data used by the audit flow experience.
  repoLibrary: null,

  // Indicates whether the user has actively selected an audit.
  auditSelected: false,

  // Startup readiness is independent from audit selection.
  startupReady: false,

  // Browser-local recovery is inspected at startup and applied only by user action.
  recoveryCandidate: null,

  // Prevents duplicate New Audit activation while POST /api/audits is pending.
  creatingAudit: false,

  // Current active audit selector value.
  activeAuditValue: "",

  // Temporary audit selector value used while an audit switch is pending.
  pendingActiveAuditValue: "",

  // Baseline active audit value used to detect or restore switch state.
  activeAuditBaseline: "",

  // Prevents duplicate or conflicting audit-switch operations while one is running.
  auditSwitchInProgress: false,
  databaseAudits: [],
  deletedAudits: [],
  deletedAuditsStatus: "idle",
  deletedAuditsError: "",
  // My Audits view state. Assigned is the default for each application load.
  myAuditsRoleFilter: "assigned",
  myAuditsStatusFilter: "all",
  myAuditsSearch: "",
  auditPersistence: {
    auditId: null,
    revisionId: null,
    revisionNumber: 0,
    access: null,
    saving: false,
    conflict: null,
  },

  // Stores the preferred field display mode, defaulting to source-based display.
  // The value is persisted in localStorage so the preference survives page reloads.
  fieldDisplayMode:
    localStorage.getItem("atlasAlignFieldDisplayMode") || "source",

  // Requirements Preview display mode. Nested is the default; complete is display-only.
  previewStatementMode:
    localStorage.getItem("atlasAlignPreviewStatementMode") || "nested",
};

const e = {
  // Audit metadata inputs.
  auditName: $("auditName"),
  auditPurpose: $("auditPurpose"),
  auditStatus: $("auditStatus"),

  // Primary list containers for audit configuration sections.

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// UI feedback and theme state
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Displays a status message in the app status area.
 * @param {any} message - Status or validation message.
 * @param {any} kind - Picker or record kind.
 */
function status(message, kind = "") {
  if (!e.status) return;
  e.status.innerHTML = `<div class="status ${esc(kind)}">${esc(message)}</div>`;
}

/**
 * Returns the active light/dark theme.
 */
function themeMode() {
  return document.body.classList.contains("dark") ? "dark" : "light";
}

/**
 * Applies the requested light/dark theme and persists the preference.
 * @param {any} mode - Requested mode value.
 */
function setTheme(mode) {
  const normalizedMode = mode === "dark" ? "dark" : "light";
  document.body.classList.toggle("dark", normalizedMode === "dark");
  if (e.theme) e.theme.textContent = normalizedMode === "dark" ? "☀" : "⏾";
  localStorage.setItem("atlasAlignTheme", normalizedMode);
  if (typeof scheduleAutosave === "function")
    scheduleAutosave({ database: false });
}

/**
 * Returns whether an audit has been selected or created.
 */
function atlasSetApplicationReady(ready) {
  state.startupReady = Boolean(ready);
  document.body.dataset.atlasReady = state.startupReady ? "true" : "false";
  if (state.startupReady)
    document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("atlas:ready"));
}

function hasSelectedAudit() {
  return !!state.auditSelected;
}

/**
 * Marks the app as having an active audit and unlocks audit pages.
 */
function markAuditSelected() {
  state.auditSelected = true;
  document.body.classList.add("audit-selected");
  document.body.classList.remove("audit-not-selected");
  updateNavigationLockState?.();
}

/**
 * Marks the app as not having an active audit and locks audit pages.
 */
function clearAuditSelection() {
  state.auditSelected = false;
  document.body.classList.remove("audit-selected");
  document.body.classList.add("audit-not-selected");
  updateNavigationLockState?.();
}

/**
 * Captures the current audit state as a JSON string for change detection.
 */
function auditSnapshot() {
  capture?.();
  return JSON.stringify(state.audit || {});
}

/**
 * Stores the current audit snapshot as the clean baseline.
 */
function updateActiveAuditBaseline() {
  state.activeAuditBaseline = auditSnapshot();
}

/**
 * Compares the current audit snapshot to the saved baseline.
 */
function auditHasUnsavedChanges() {
  if (!hasSelectedAudit()) return false;
  return auditSnapshot() !== state.activeAuditBaseline;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Field display mode helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Returns whether raw source names or vanity labels should be displayed.
 */
function fieldDisplayMode() {
  return state.fieldDisplayMode === "vanity" ? "vanity" : "source";
}

/**
 * Finds the catalog field record that matches a raw or display field name.
 * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
 * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
 */
function findFieldDisplayCandidate(value, sourceFile = "") {
  const text = String(value || "");
  if (!text) return null;
  const pools = [];
  if (typeof fieldsForSource === "function")
    pools.push(...fieldsForSource(sourceFile || ""));
  pools.push(...((atlasDisplayDocument?.() || state.audit).fields || []));
  return (
    pools.find((field) =>
      [
        field.fieldName,
        field.rawFieldName,
        field.name,
        field.label,
        field.vanityLabel,
      ].some((item) => item && String(item) === text),
    ) || null
  );
}

/**
 * Returns the field name to show based on the active display mode.
 * @param {any} fieldOrName - Input used by fieldDisplayName.
 * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
 */
function fieldDisplayParts(fieldOrName, sourceFile = "") {
  const fallback = String(typeof fieldOrName === "string" ? fieldOrName : "");
  if (!fieldOrName) return { raw: "", vanity: "", display: "", alternate: "" };
  const field =
    typeof fieldOrName === "string"
      ? findFieldDisplayCandidate(fieldOrName, sourceFile)
      : fieldOrName;
  if (!field)
    return {
      raw: fallback,
      vanity: fallback,
      display: fallback,
      alternate: "",
    };
  const raw = String(
    field.fieldName ||
      field.rawFieldName ||
      fallback ||
      field.name ||
      field.label ||
      "",
  );
  const vanity = String(
    field.vanityLabel ||
      (field.name && field.name !== raw ? field.name : "") ||
      field.label ||
      raw,
  );
  const display =
    fieldDisplayMode() === "vanity" ? vanity || raw : raw || vanity;
  const alternate =
    fieldDisplayMode() === "vanity"
      ? raw !== display
        ? raw
        : ""
      : vanity !== display
        ? vanity
        : "";
  return { raw, vanity, display, alternate };
}

function fieldDisplayName(fieldOrName, sourceFile = "") {
  return fieldDisplayParts(fieldOrName, sourceFile).display;
}

/**
 * Updates and persists the field display mode, then re-renders the UI.
 * @param {any} mode - Requested mode value.
 */
function setFieldDisplayMode(mode) {
  state.fieldDisplayMode = mode === "vanity" ? "vanity" : "source";
  localStorage.setItem("atlasAlignFieldDisplayMode", state.fieldDisplayMode);
  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
  renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
}

/**
 * Switches between raw/source and vanity-label display modes.
 */
function toggleFieldDisplayMode() {
  setFieldDisplayMode(fieldDisplayMode() === "vanity" ? "source" : "vanity");
}

/**
 * Maps legacy section names to current page ids.
 * @param {any} section - Section/page identifier.
 */
function pageForSection(section) {
  if (section === "audit") return "overview";
  if (section === "logic") return "steps";
  if (section === "publication") return "save";
  if (section === "sources") return "fields";
  return section || "my-audits";
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Page navigation helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Activates a page, updates navigation state, and refreshes page-level controls.
 * @param {any} section - Section/page identifier.
 */
function showSection(section) {
  // Resolve the requested section into the canonical page name used by the app.
  let page = pageForSection(section);

  // If no audit is selected, keep users on an allowed landing/setup page.
  // This prevents navigation into audit-specific pages before an audit context exists.
  if (!hasSelectedAudit() && page !== "my-audits" && page !== "group")
    page = "my-audits";

  // Update the active state for navigation buttons in both the top nav and rail.
  // Each button may identify its target through section, page, or panel metadata.
  document.querySelectorAll(".navitem, .rail-item").forEach((button) => {
    const buttonPage = pageForSection(
      button.dataset.section ||
        button.dataset.page ||
        button.dataset.panel ||
        "",
    );
    button.classList.toggle("active", buttonPage === page);
  });

  // Show the matching main page panel and hide all other page panels.
  // Supports matching by data-page, page-prefixed id, or direct id for compatibility.
  document.querySelectorAll(".page").forEach((panel) => {
    panel.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      panel.dataset.page === page ||
        panel.id === `page-${page}` ||
        panel.id === page,
    );
  });

  // Show the matching setup panel when the selected page maps to setup content.
  document.querySelectorAll(".setup-panel").forEach((panel) => {
    panel.classList.toggle("active", panel.dataset.panel === page);
  });

  // Persist the active section so the app can restore the user's last location later.
  localStorage.setItem("atlasAlignActiveSection", page);

  // Refresh page-dependent UI elements after navigation changes.
  // Function checks keep this safe when optional modules are not loaded.
  if (typeof updateDashboardMetrics === "function") updateDashboardMetrics();
  if (
    page === "preview" &&
    typeof atlasPreviewDirty !== "undefined" &&
    atlasPreviewDirty
  ) {
    atlasRequestRender?.({ preview: true });
  }
  if (typeof positionTutorial === "function") positionTutorial();
  updateNavigationLockState?.();
  renderFlowNavControls?.();
  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
  renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
  atlasRenderPage?.(page);
  globalThis.ATLASAuditIssues?.render?.();

  // Return the viewport to the top of the newly displayed section.
  window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "auto" });
}

// Package 2D.3 detached display-document provider. Editable state.audit remains untouched.
function atlasIsReviewerMode() {
  return Boolean(window.ATLASAuditReview?.isActive?.());
}
function atlasReviewerModeDocument() {
  return window.ATLASAuditReview?.document?.() || null;
}
function atlasDisplayDocument() {
  return typeof window.atlasCurrentRenderDocument === "function"
    ? window.atlasCurrentRenderDocument()
    : atlasReviewerModeDocument() || state.audit;
}
function atlasReviewerModeContext() {
  return window.ATLASAuditReview?.context?.() || null;
}
