/**
 * 07-phrase-catalog.js
 *
 * Phrase catalog loading, formatting helpers, and analyst/developer logic generation for standardized audit language.
 * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
 */

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Phrase catalog state
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
const REQUIRED_CATALOG_KEYS = [
  "version",
  "description",
  "auto_note_prefixes",
  "notes",
  "format_field",
  "list_format",
  "developer_logic",
  "analyst_logic",
];
let phraseCatalog = {};
const missingPhrasePaths = new Set();
const catalogLoadWarnings = [];

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Catalog loading and lookup
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Loads and validates the phrase catalog JSON.
 */
async function initPhraseCatalog() {
  missingPhrasePaths.clear();
  catalogLoadWarnings.length = 0;
  try {
    const response = await fetch("logic_phrase_catalog.json", {
      cache: "no-store",
    });
    if (!response.ok)
      throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    const json = await response.json();
    validateCatalogShape(json);
    phraseCatalog = json;
    status("Phrase catalog loaded from logic_phrase_catalog.json.", "ok");
  } catch (error) {
    phraseCatalog = {};
    catalogLoadWarnings.push(
      `Phrase catalog could not be loaded: ${error.message}`,
    );
    status(
      "Phrase catalog could not be loaded. Analyst Logic will show missing phrase warnings.",
      "bad",
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Checks that the phrase catalog has required top-level keys.
 * @param {any} catalog - Input used by validateCatalogShape.
 */
function validateCatalogShape(catalog) {
  if (!catalog || typeof catalog !== "object" || Array.isArray(catalog))
    throw new Error("logic_phrase_catalog.json must contain a JSON object.");
  const missing = REQUIRED_CATALOG_KEYS.filter((key) => !(key in catalog));
  if (missing.length)
    throw new Error(
      `logic_phrase_catalog.json is missing required top-level key(s): ${missing.join(", ")}.`,
    );
}

/**
 * Reads a nested value from the phrase catalog.
 * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
 * @param {any} defaultValue - Fallback value when a catalog path is missing.
 */
function getCatalogValue(path, defaultValue = undefined) {
  if (!path) return defaultValue;
  let current = phraseCatalog;
  for (const part of String(path).split(".")) {
    if (
      current &&
      typeof current === "object" &&
      Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(current, part)
    )
      current = current[part];
    else {
      missingPhrasePaths.add(path);
      return defaultValue;
    }
  }
  return current;
}

/**
 * Reads a string template from the phrase catalog.
 * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
 */
function phraseTemplate(path) {
  const value = getCatalogValue(path, undefined);
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    missingPhrasePaths.add(path);
    return "";
  }
  return value;
}

/**
 * Substitutes template placeholders with values.
 * @param {any} template - Input used by renderPhraseFromTemplate.
 * @param {any} values - Input used by renderPhraseFromTemplate.
 */
function renderPhraseFromTemplate(template, values = {}) {
  return String(template || "").replace(
    /\{([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)\}/g,
    (match, key) => {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(values, key))
        return String(values[key] ?? "");
      return match;
    },
  );
}

/**
 * Renders a phrase template by catalog path.
 * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
 * @param {any} values - Input used by renderCatalogPhrase.
 */
function renderCatalogPhrase(path, values = {}) {
  const template = phraseTemplate(path);
  if (!template) return `[Missing phrase path: ${path}]`;
  return renderPhraseFromTemplate(template, values);
}

/**
 * Reads an array from the phrase catalog using an options object.
 * Explicit property names keep call sites readable and avoid positional-argument ambiguity.
 * @param {Object} options - Catalog-array lookup options.
 * @param {string} options.path - Catalog path to read.
 * @param {Array<any>} options.fallback - Array returned when the catalog value is missing or is not an array.
 */
function catalogArrayFromOptions({ path = "", fallback = [] } = {}) {
  const value = getCatalogValue(path, undefined);
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value : fallback;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Phrase formatting helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Quotes a value for standardized analyst logic.
 * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
 */
function quotePhraseValue(value) {
  const clean = String(value ?? "").trim();
  return clean ? `"${clean}"` : '""';
}

/**
 * Resolves a source alias from audit, repo, or workbook metadata.
 * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
 */
function sourceAliasFor(sourceFile) {
  const fromState = (state.audit.sources || []).find(
    (source) => source.name === sourceFile,
  )?.alias;
  if (fromState) return fromState;
  const fromRepo = (repoLibraryState?.sourceAliases || []).find(
    (item) => item.sourceFile === sourceFile,
  )?.alias;
  if (fromRepo) return fromRepo;
  const fromWorkbook = (
    typeof workbookState !== "undefined"
      ? workbookState.sourceAliases || []
      : []
  ).find((item) => item.sourceFile === sourceFile)?.alias;
  return fromWorkbook || sourceFile || "";
}

/**
 * Formats a source/field pair as standardized column text.
 * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
 * @param {any} alias - Input used by tableColumnPlain.
 * @param {any} fieldName - Raw field name.
 * @param {any} omitSource - Input used by tableColumnPlain.
 */
function tableColumnPlain(sourceFile, alias, fieldName, omitSource = false) {
  const field = String(fieldName || "").trim();
  if (!field) return "";
  const source = String(sourceFile || "").trim();
  const resolvedAlias = String(
    alias || sourceAliasFor(source) || source,
  ).trim();
  if (
    source === "First Available Value" ||
    resolvedAlias === "First Available Value"
  )
    return field;
  if (
    source === "Calculated Field" ||
    resolvedAlias === "Calculated Field" ||
    source === "Created Columns"
  )
    return `new column ${quotePhraseValue(field)}`;
  if (omitSource || !resolvedAlias) return `column ${quotePhraseValue(field)}`;
  return `file ${quotePhraseValue(resolvedAlias)} column ${quotePhraseValue(field)}`;
}

/**
 * Formats a list of comparison values using catalog list templates.
 * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
 */
function formattedValueList(value) {
  const values = Array.isArray(value)
    ? value
    : String(value ?? "")
        .split(/\r?\n/)
        .map((item) => item.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
  const quoted = values.map(quotePhraseValue);
  if (quoted.length === 0) return "";
  if (quoted.length === 1) return quoted[0];
  if (quoted.length === 2)
    return renderCatalogPhrase("list_format.two_item_template", {
      first: quoted[0],
      second: quoted[1],
    });
  return renderCatalogPhrase("list_format.many_item_template", {
    items: quoted.slice(0, -1).join(", "),
    last: quoted.at(-1),
  });
}

function conditionCompareMathOperationsForPhrase(condition = {}) {
  if (typeof parseSourceMathOperations !== "function") return [];
  return parseSourceMathOperations(
    condition.compareMathJson ||
      condition.compare_math_json ||
      condition.compareMath ||
      [],
  );
}

function compareMathOperatorWord(operator = "") {
  if (operator === "+") return "plus";
  if (operator === "-") return "minus";
  if (operator === "*") return "times";
  if (operator === "/") return "divided by";
  return "";
}

function compareMathOperandPlain(operation = {}, fallbackSourceFile = "") {
  if ((operation.operandType || "number") === "field") {
    const sourceFile = operation.operandSourceFile || fallbackSourceFile;
    const alias = operation.operandSourceAlias || sourceAliasFor(sourceFile);
    return tableColumnPlain(
      sourceFile,
      alias,
      operation.operandFieldName || "",
    );
  }
  return String(operation.operand || "").trim();
}

function conditionCompareFieldPlain(condition = {}) {
  const base = tableColumnPlain(
    condition.compareSourceFile || condition.sourceFile,
    condition.compareSourceAlias || condition.sourceAlias,
    condition.compareFieldName,
  );
  const operations = conditionCompareMathOperationsForPhrase(condition);
  const mathText = operations
    .map((operation) => {
      const word = compareMathOperatorWord(operation.operator);
      const operand = compareMathOperandPlain(
        operation,
        condition.compareSourceFile || condition.sourceFile,
      );
      return word && operand ? `${word} ${operand}` : "";
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ");
  return `${base}${mathText ? ` ${mathText}` : ""}`.trim();
}

/**
 * Formats the right side of a condition.
 * @param {any} condition - Condition object to format or render.
 */
function conditionValuePlain(condition) {
  if (condition.valueMode === "blank") return "blank/null";
  if (condition.valueMode === "field")
    return conditionCompareFieldPlain(condition);
  if (condition.valueMode === "expression")
    return String(condition.expression || "").trim();
  return formattedValueList(condition.compareValue || condition.value || "");
}

/**
 * Formats a full condition as plain standardized text.
 * @param {any} condition - Condition object to format or render.
 * @param {any} omitSource - Input used by conditionToPlain.
 */
function conditionToPlain(condition, omitSource = false) {
  const left = tableColumnPlain(
    condition.sourceFile,
    condition.sourceAlias,
    condition.fieldName || condition.field,
    omitSource,
  );
  const operator =
    condition.valueMode === "blank"
      ? String(condition.operator || "").includes("not")
        ? "is not blank/null"
        : "is blank/null"
      : condition.operator || "is equal to";
  const value =
    condition.valueMode === "blank" ? "" : conditionValuePlain(condition);
  return `${left} ${operator}${value ? ` ${value}` : ""}`.trim();
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Condition normalization and analyst logic
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Normalizes stored condition shapes into guided condition objects.
 * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
 */
function parseStepConditions(step) {
  const params = step.params || {};
  if (params.conditions_json) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(params.conditions_json);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;
    } catch {}
  }
  return (step.conditions || []).map((condition, index) => ({
    id: condition.id || uid("cond"),
    connector: index === 0 ? "IF" : condition.connector || "AND",
    sourceFile: condition.sourceFile || step.source || params.source_file || "",
    sourceAlias:
      condition.sourceAlias ||
      params.source_alias ||
      sourceAliasFor(
        condition.sourceFile || step.source || params.source_file || "",
      ),
    fieldName:
      condition.fieldName || condition.field || params.field_name || "",
    operator: condition.operator || params.operator || "is equal to",
    valueMode: condition.valueMode || "values",
    compareValue:
      condition.compareValue || condition.value || params.compare_value || "",
    compareSourceFile: condition.compareSourceFile || "",
    compareSourceAlias: condition.compareSourceAlias || "",
    compareFieldName: condition.compareFieldName || "",
    expression: condition.expression || "",
    compareMathJson:
      condition.compareMathJson || condition.compare_math_json || "",
  }));
}

const INVERSE_CONDITION_OPERATOR_MAP = {
  "is equal to": "is not equal to",
  "is not equal to": "is equal to",
  contains: "does not contain",
  "does not contain": "contains",
  "starts with": "does not start with",
  "does not start with": "starts with",
  "ends with": "does not end with",
  "does not end with": "ends with",
  "is greater than": "is less than or equal to",
  "is greater than or equal to": "is less than",
  "is less than": "is greater than or equal to",
  "is less than or equal to": "is greater than",
  "is blank/null": "is not blank/null",
  "is not blank/null": "is blank/null",
  "matches expression": "does not match expression",
  "does not match expression": "matches expression",
};

/**
 * Returns the logical opposite of a guided IF operator.
 * @param {string} operator - Operator label from the IF condition editor.
 * @param {string} valueMode - Condition value mode, used for blank/null defaults.
 */
function inverseConditionOperator(operator, valueMode = "values") {
  const normalized = String(operator || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
  if (INVERSE_CONDITION_OPERATOR_MAP[normalized])
    return INVERSE_CONDITION_OPERATOR_MAP[normalized];
  if (valueMode === "blank")
    return normalized.includes("not") ? "is blank/null" : "is not blank/null";
  if (normalized.startsWith("does not "))
    return normalized.replace(/^does not\s+/, "");
  if (normalized.startsWith("does "))
    return normalized.replace(/^does\s+/, "does not ");
  if (normalized.startsWith("is not "))
    return normalized.replace(/^is not\s+/, "is ");
  if (normalized.startsWith("is "))
    return normalized.replace(/^is\s+/, "is not ");
  // Unknown operators are left unchanged because the app cannot infer a safe inverse.
  return operator || "is equal to";
}

/**
 * Returns the De Morgan opposite of a condition connector.
 * @param {string} connector - Connector from a guided condition row.
 */
function inverseConditionConnector(connector) {
  const normalized = String(connector || "")
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();
  if (normalized === "AND") return "OR";
  if (normalized === "OR") return "AND";
  return "AND";
}

/**
 * Creates the inverse of one guided condition while preserving source/value metadata.
 * @param {any} condition - Guided condition object from parseStepConditions.
 * @param {number} index - Zero-based condition index.
 */
function invertGuidedCondition(condition, index = 0) {
  const next = {
    ...condition,
    id: uid("cond"),
    connector:
      index === 0 ? "IF" : inverseConditionConnector(condition.connector),
    operator: inverseConditionOperator(condition.operator, condition.valueMode),
  };
  if ((next.valueMode || "values") === "blank") {
    next.compareValue = "";
    next.compareFieldName = "";
    next.expression = "";
  }
  return next;
}

/**
 * Builds inverted conditions for a complete IF step.
 * @param {any} step - IF step to invert.
 */
function invertedConditionsForStep(step) {
  const parsed = parseStepConditions(step);
  const conditions = parsed.length
    ? parsed
    : typeof defaultGuidedCondition === "function"
      ? [defaultGuidedCondition(step)]
      : [];
  return conditions.map((condition, index) =>
    invertGuidedCondition(condition, index),
  );
}

/**
 * Formats all IF/THEN conditions as a condition group.
 * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
 */
function conditionGroupPlain(step) {
  return parseStepConditions(step)
    .map((condition, index) => {
      const connector = index === 0 ? "IF" : condition.connector || "AND";
      return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.if_then.condition_line", {
        connector,
        condition_text: conditionToPlain(condition, false),
      });
    })
    .join(" ")
    .trim();
}

/**
 * Ensures condition text starts with an IF-style prefix.
 * @param {any} text - Input used by ensureIfThenPrefix.
 */
function ensureIfThenPrefix(text) {
  const value = String(text || "").trim();
  if (!value) return value;
  if (/^(IF|ELSE IF|ELSE)\b/i.test(value)) return value;
  return `IF ${value}`;
}

/**
 * Formats the THEN action for an IF/THEN step.
 * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
 */
function thenActionPlain(step) {
  const params = step.params || {};
  const actionType = params.then_action_type || "freeform";
  if (actionType === "display_text")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.if_then.then_display_text", {
      message: quotePhraseValue(params.then_text || ""),
      output_column: quotePhraseValue(
        params.then_output_column || "Error Message",
      ),
    });
  if (actionType === "set_column_value")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.if_then.then_set_column_value", {
      target_column: quotePhraseValue(params.then_target_column || ""),
      value_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.then_value_source_file || params.source_file,
        params.then_value_source_alias || params.source_alias,
        params.then_value_field || "",
      ),
    });
  if (actionType === "do_not_display")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.if_then.then_do_not_display", {});
  return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.if_then.then_freeform", {
    action: params.then_action || step.action || "",
  });
}

/**
 * Formats an ELSE step action.
 * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
 */
function elseActionPlain(step) {
  const params = step.params || {};
  const actionType = params.then_action_type || "freeform";
  if (actionType === "display_text")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.else.display_text", {
      message: quotePhraseValue(params.then_text || ""),
      output_column: quotePhraseValue(
        params.then_output_column || "Error Message",
      ),
    });
  if (actionType === "set_column_value")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.else.set_column_value", {
      target_column: quotePhraseValue(params.then_target_column || ""),
      value_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.then_value_source_file || params.source_file,
        params.then_value_source_alias || params.source_alias,
        params.then_value_field || "",
      ),
    });
  if (actionType === "do_not_display")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.else.do_not_display", {});
  if (params.then_action)
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.else.freeform", {
      action: params.then_action,
    });
  return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.else.default", {});
}

/**
 * Selects the phrase path for a Create Column step.
 * @param {any} params - Step params object.
 */
function createColumnPhrasePath(params) {
  if (params.value_mode === "Static value")
    return "analyst_logic.create_column.static_value";
  if (params.value_mode === "Source field value")
    return "analyst_logic.create_column.source_field";
  if (params.value_mode === "Concatenate fields")
    return "analyst_logic.create_column.concatenate_fields";
  if (params.expression_type === "first_characters")
    return "analyst_logic.create_column.first_characters";
  if (params.expression_type === "character_count")
    return "analyst_logic.create_column.character_count";
  if (params.value_mode === "Expression / calculation")
    return "analyst_logic.create_column.expression";
  return "analyst_logic.create_column.default";
}

/**
 * Returns true when a join type represents a Union.
 * @param {any} joinType - Input used by phraseIsUnionJoinType.
 */
function phraseIsUnionJoinType(joinType) {
  return (
    String(joinType || "")
      .trim()
      .toUpperCase() === "UNION"
  );
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Create Column source-field math and rounding phrase helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Formats the rounding precision target for analyst-facing Create Column text.
 * @param {any} decimalPlacesValue - Raw decimal places value.
 */
function atlasRoundingTargetText(decimalPlacesValue) {
  const decimals = Number.parseInt(decimalPlacesValue, 10);
  const safeDecimals =
    Number.isFinite(decimals) && decimals >= 0 ? decimals : 0;
  if (safeDecimals === 0) return "nearest whole number";
  return `${safeDecimals} decimal place${safeDecimals === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
}

/**
 * Formats the rounding direction phrase for analyst-facing Create Column text.
 * @param {string} roundingMode - Rounding mode.
 */
function atlasRoundingDirectionText(roundingMode) {
  if (roundingMode === "down") return "down to";
  if (roundingMode === "up") return "up to";
  return "to";
}

function atlasReadableSourceFieldName(p = {}) {
  return (
    quotePhraseValue?.(p.field_name || "") || `"${esc(p.field_name || "")}"`
  );
}

function atlasSourceMathOperationPhrase(operation, index, sourceField) {
  const operand = String(operation?.operand || "").trim();
  if (!operand) return "";
  if (index === 0) {
    if (operation.operator === "+")
      return `adding ${operand} to column ${sourceField}`;
    if (operation.operator === "-")
      return `subtracting ${operand} from column ${sourceField}`;
    if (operation.operator === "*")
      return `multiplying column ${sourceField} by ${operand}`;
    if (operation.operator === "/")
      return `dividing column ${sourceField} by ${operand}`;
    return "";
  }
  if (operation.operator === "+") return `adding ${operand}`;
  if (operation.operator === "-") return `subtracting ${operand}`;
  if (operation.operator === "*") return `multiplying the result by ${operand}`;
  if (operation.operator === "/") return `dividing by ${operand}`;
  return "";
}

function atlasJoinReadableList(items = []) {
  const filtered = items.filter(Boolean);
  if (!filtered.length) return "";
  if (filtered.length === 1) return filtered[0];
  if (filtered.length === 2) return `${filtered[0]} or ${filtered[1]}`;
  return `${filtered.slice(0, -1).join(", ")}, or ${filtered.at(-1)}`;
}

function atlasSourceFieldMathPhrase(p = {}) {
  const sourceField = atlasReadableSourceFieldName(p);
  const operations =
    typeof parseSourceMathOperations === "function"
      ? parseSourceMathOperations(p)
      : [];
  return atlasJoinReadableList(
    operations.map((operation, index) =>
      atlasSourceMathOperationPhrase(operation, index, sourceField),
    ),
  );
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Step-to-analyst-logic renderer
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Generates standardized analyst-facing logic text for a step.
 * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
 */
function analystLogic(step) {
  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
  const params = step.params || {};
  const stepType = step.step_type;
  if (stepType === "reference_source") {
    const sourceFile = params.source_file || step.source || "";
    const alias = params.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(sourceFile);
    return alias && alias !== sourceFile
      ? renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.reference_source.with_alias", {
          source_file: quotePhraseValue(sourceFile),
          source_alias: quotePhraseValue(alias),
        })
      : renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.reference_source.without_alias", {
          source_file: quotePhraseValue(sourceFile),
        });
  }
  if (stepType === "create_column") {
    const columnName = params.column_name || step.field || "";
    if (
      (params.value_mode === "Static value" || !params.value_mode) &&
      !String(params.static_value || "").trim()
    ) {
      return `Create new blank column ${quotePhraseValue(columnName)}.`;
    }
    const tableColumn = tableColumnPlain(
      params.source_file,
      params.source_alias,
      params.field_name,
    );
    return renderCatalogPhrase(createColumnPhrasePath(params), {
      column_name: quotePhraseValue(columnName),
      static_value: quotePhraseValue(params.static_value || ""),
      table_column: tableColumn,
      expression: params.expression || "",
      character_count: params.character_count || "",
      first_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.concat_source_1 || params.source_file,
        sourceAliasFor(params.concat_source_1 || params.source_file),
        params.concat_field_1,
      ),
      second_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.concat_source_2 || params.source_file,
        sourceAliasFor(params.concat_source_2 || params.source_file),
        params.concat_field_2,
      ),
    });
  }
  if (stepType === "if_then") {
    const group = ensureIfThenPrefix(conditionGroupPlain(step));
    const action = thenActionPlain(step);
    const phrase = action
      ? renderCatalogPhrase(
          "analyst_logic.if_then.condition_group_with_action",
          { condition_group: group, then_action: action },
        )
      : renderCatalogPhrase(
          "analyst_logic.if_then.condition_group_without_action",
          { condition_group: group },
        );
    return ensureIfThenPrefix(phrase);
  }
  if (stepType === "else") return elseActionPlain(step);
  if (stepType === "join") {
    if (phraseIsUnionJoinType(params.join_type))
      return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.union_sources.default", {
        left_source: quotePhraseValue(
          params.left_source || params.union_left_source || "",
        ),
        right_source: quotePhraseValue(
          params.right_source || params.union_right_source || "",
        ),
        output_source: quotePhraseValue(
          params.output_source || step.source || "",
        ),
      });
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.join.default", {
      left_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.left_source,
        params.left_alias,
        params.left_field,
      ),
      right_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.right_source,
        params.right_alias,
        params.right_field,
      ),
    });
  }
  if (stepType === "union_sources")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.union_sources.default", {
      left_source: quotePhraseValue(params.union_left_source || ""),
      right_source: quotePhraseValue(params.union_right_source || ""),
      output_source: quotePhraseValue(
        params.output_source || step.source || "",
      ),
    });
  if (stepType === "format_field")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.format_field.default", {
      table_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.source_file,
        params.source_alias,
        params.field_name,
      ),
      format: params.format || "",
      format_suffix: "",
    });
  if (stepType === "split_field")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.split_field.default", {
      table_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.source_file,
        params.source_alias,
        params.field_name,
      ),
      split_on: quotePhraseValue(params.split_on || ""),
      split_part: params.split_part || "",
      output_column: quotePhraseValue(params.output_column || ""),
    });
  if (stepType === "sort_records")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.sort_records.default", {
      table_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.source_file,
        params.source_alias,
        params.field_name,
      ),
      sort_direction: params.sort_direction || "Ascending",
    });
  if (stepType === "remove_duplicates")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.remove_duplicates.default", {
      table_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.source_file,
        params.source_alias,
        params.field_name,
      ),
      duplicate_action: params.duplicate_action || "Keep first record",
    });
  if (stepType === "filter_rows")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.filter_rows.default", {
      table_column: tableColumnPlain(
        params.source_file,
        params.source_alias,
        params.field_name,
      ),
      operator: params.operator || "is equal to",
      compare_value: params.compare_value
        ? ` ${formattedValueList(params.compare_value)}`
        : "",
      filter_action: params.filter_action || "Keep matching rows",
    });
  if (stepType === "flag_records")
    return `${renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.flag_records.condition", { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), operator: params.operator || "is equal to", compare_value: params.compare_value ? ` ${formattedValueList(params.compare_value)}` : "" })} ${params.flag_action === "do_not_display" ? renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.flag_records.do_not_display", {}) : renderCatalogPhrase("analyst_logic.flag_records.display_text", { message: quotePhraseValue(params.message || ""), output_column: quotePhraseValue(params.output_column || "Error Message") })}`;
  return `[Missing phrase path: analyst_logic.${stepType || "unknown"}]`;
}

/**
 * Generates notes for a step when manual notes are not provided.
 * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
 * @param {any} index - Zero-based index used for ordering or display.
 */
function noteText(step, index) {
  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
  const params = step.params || {};
  if (step.notes) return step.notes;
  if (step.step_type === "join") {
    if (phraseIsUnionJoinType(params.join_type))
      return renderCatalogPhrase("notes.union", {
        left_source:
          params.left_source || params.union_left_source || "[file 1]",
        right_source:
          params.right_source || params.union_right_source || "[file 2]",
        output_source: params.output_source || "[combined file]",
      });
    return renderCatalogPhrase(`notes.join.${params.join_type || "DEFAULT"}`, {
      left_alias: params.left_alias || params.left_source || "[file 1]",
      right_alias: params.right_alias || params.right_source || "[file 2]",
      keys: `${quotePhraseValue(params.left_field || "[file 1 key]")} / ${quotePhraseValue(params.right_field || "[file 2 key]")}`,
    });
  }
  if (step.step_type === "union_sources")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("notes.union", {
      left_source: params.union_left_source || "[file 1]",
      right_source: params.union_right_source || "[file 2]",
      output_source: params.output_source || "[combined file]",
    });
  if (step.step_type === "else")
    return renderCatalogPhrase("notes.else.default", {});
  return ``;
}

// ATLAS-MATH-OPERATIONS-UPDATE:BEGIN
atlasSourceMathOperationPhrase =
  function atlasSourceMathOperationPhraseCanonical(
    operation,
    index,
    sourceField,
  ) {
    const operand = compareMathOperandPlain(
      operation,
      operation.operandSourceFile || "",
    );
    if (!operand) return "";
    if (index === 0) {
      if (operation.operator === "+")
        return `adding ${operand} to column ${sourceField}`;
      if (operation.operator === "-")
        return `subtracting ${operand} from column ${sourceField}`;
      if (operation.operator === "*")
        return `multiplying column ${sourceField} by ${operand}`;
      if (operation.operator === "/")
        return `dividing column ${sourceField} by ${operand}`;
    }
    if (operation.operator === "+") return `adding ${operand}`;
    if (operation.operator === "-") return `subtracting ${operand}`;
    if (operation.operator === "*")
      return `multiplying the result by ${operand}`;
    if (operation.operator === "/") return `dividing the result by ${operand}`;
    return "";
  };

function atlasMathRoundingPhrase(mode = "none", decimals = "0") {
  if (!mode || mode === "none") return "";
  return `, then round ${atlasRoundingDirectionText(mode)} the ${atlasRoundingTargetText(decimals)}`;
}

const atlasThenActionPlainBeforeMathUpdate = thenActionPlain;
thenActionPlain = function thenActionPlainWithMath(step) {
  const p = step.params || {};
  if (p.then_action_type !== "set_column_calculation")
    return atlasThenActionPlainBeforeMathUpdate(step);
  const source = tableColumnPlain(
    p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file,
    p.then_value_source_alias ||
      sourceAliasFor(p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file),
    p.then_value_field,
  );
  const operations = parseSourceMathOperations(p.then_math_json || []);
  const math = operations
    .map(
      (operation) =>
        `${compareMathOperatorWord(operation.operator)} ${compareMathOperandPlain(operation, p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}`,
    )
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ");
  return renderCatalogPhrase(
    "analyst_logic.if_then.then_set_column_calculation",
    {
      target_column: quotePhraseValue(p.then_target_column || ""),
      calculation: `${source}${math ? ` ${math}` : ""}${atlasMathRoundingPhrase(p.then_rounding_mode, p.then_rounding_decimal_places)}`,
    },
  );
};

const atlasAnalystLogicBeforeMathUpdate = analystLogic;
analystLogic = function analystLogicWithStructuredMath(step) {
  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
  const p = step.params || {};
  if (
    step.step_type === "create_column" &&
    p.value_mode === "Source field value" &&
    (parseSourceMathOperations(p.source_math_json || []).length ||
      (p.rounding_mode && p.rounding_mode !== "none"))
  ) {
    const base = tableColumnPlain(p.source_file, p.source_alias, p.field_name);
    const operations = parseSourceMathOperations(p.source_math_json || []);
    const math = operations
      .map(
        (operation) =>
          `${compareMathOperatorWord(operation.operator)} ${compareMathOperandPlain(operation, p.source_file)}`,
      )
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" ");
    return renderCatalogPhrase(
      "analyst_logic.create_column.source_field_calculation",
      {
        column_name: quotePhraseValue(p.column_name || step.field || ""),
        calculation: `${base}${math ? ` ${math}` : ""}${atlasMathRoundingPhrase(p.rounding_mode, p.rounding_decimal_places)}`,
      },
    );
  }
  return atlasAnalystLogicBeforeMathUpdate(step);
};
// ATLAS-MATH-OPERATIONS-UPDATE:END

// ATLAS-STRING-TRANSFORM-UPDATE:BEGIN
function atlasStringTransformAnalystValues(config) {
  return {
    target_column: quotePhraseValue(config.destinationColumn),
    source_column: tableColumnPlain(
      config.sourceFile,
      config.sourceAlias || sourceAliasFor(config.sourceFile),
      config.sourceField,
    ),
    position: config.position,
    character_count:
      atlasPositiveWholeNumber(config.count) || "[positive whole number]",
  };
}
function atlasStringTransformAnalystPhrase(config, context = "create_column") {
  const suffix =
    config.operation === "character_count" ? "string_length" : "substring";
  return renderCatalogPhrase(
    `analyst_logic.${context}.${suffix}`,
    atlasStringTransformAnalystValues(config),
  );
}
const atlasThenActionPlainBeforeStringTransform = thenActionPlain;
thenActionPlain = function thenActionPlainWithStringTransform(step) {
  const p = step.params || {};
  if (p.then_action_type === "set_column_string_transform")
    return atlasStringTransformAnalystPhrase(
      atlasStringTransformConfig(p, "then_"),
      "if_then",
    );
  return atlasThenActionPlainBeforeStringTransform(step);
};
const atlasElseActionPlainBeforeStringTransform = elseActionPlain;
elseActionPlain = function elseActionPlainWithStringTransform(step) {
  const p = step.params || {};
  if (p.then_action_type === "set_column_string_transform")
    return `ELSE ${atlasStringTransformAnalystPhrase(atlasStringTransformConfig(p, "then_"), "if_then")}.`;
  return atlasElseActionPlainBeforeStringTransform(step);
};
const atlasAnalystLogicBeforeStringTransform = analystLogic;
analystLogic = function analystLogicWithStringTransform(step) {
  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
  if (
    step?.step_type === "create_column" &&
    step.params?.value_mode === ATLAS_STRING_TRANSFORM_VALUE_MODE
  )
    return atlasStringTransformAnalystPhrase(
      atlasStringTransformConfig(step.params),
      "create_column",
    );
  return atlasAnalystLogicBeforeStringTransform(step);
};
// ATLAS-STRING-TRANSFORM-UPDATE:END

/**
 * 08-step-templates-validation.js
 *
 * Legacy step-template helpers and validation-panel logic. Template UI may be hidden, but validation still evaluates audit completeness and step consistency.
 * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
 */

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Legacy step templates
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
const STEP_TEMPLATES = [
  {
    id: "source-review",
    label: "Source File Review",
    step: () => ({

/**
 * Populates the legacy step-template selector if present.
 */
function renderStepTemplateOptions() {
  const select = $("stepTemplateSelect");
  if (!select) return;
  select.innerHTML = STEP_TEMPLATES.map(
    (template) =>
      `<option value="${template.id}">${esc(template.label)}</option>`,
  ).join("");
}

/**
 * Inserts the selected legacy step template.
 */
function insertSelectedStepTemplate() {
  capture();
  const selected = $("stepTemplateSelect")?.value;
  const template =
    STEP_TEMPLATES.find((item) => item.id === selected) || STEP_TEMPLATES[0];
  if (!template) return;
  const step = template.step();
  state.audit.steps.push(step);
  state.selectedStepId = step.id;
  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["steps", "preview", "review"]);
  showSection("steps");
  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
  scheduleAutosave();
  status(`Inserted template: ${template.label}`, "ok");
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Audit validation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Builds validation issues for audit setup and step consistency.
 */

function atlasKnownFieldOptionValues() {
  const values = new Set();
  (state.audit.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
    if (field.name) values.add(field.name);
    if (field.rawFieldName) values.add(field.rawFieldName);
  });
  (repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles || []).forEach((source) => {
    if (source.sourceKey) values.add(source.sourceKey);
    (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
      const raw =
        field.fieldName || field.rawFieldName || field.name || field.label;
      if (raw) values.add(raw);
      if (field.vanityLabel) values.add(field.vanityLabel);
      if (field.label) values.add(field.label);
    });
  });
  return values;
}

function validateAudit() {
  return window.ATLASAuditIssues?.legacyIssues?.() || [];
}
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Validation rendering
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/** Renders the canonical issue result through the legacy Readiness panel. */
function renderValidationPanel() {
  if (window.ATLASAuditIssues?.render) {
    window.ATLASAuditIssues.render();
    return;
  }
  return atlasMeasure?.("atlas.validation", () => {
    const target = $("validationPanel");
    if (!target) return;
    const issues = validateAudit();
    if (!issues.length) {
      target.innerHTML =
        '<div class="validation-ok">No validation issues found.</div>';
      return;
    }
    target.innerHTML = `<div class="validation-list">${issues
      .map(
        (issue) =>
          `<div class="validation-item ${issue.level}"><strong>${
            issue.level === "error" ? "Error" : "Warning"
          }:</strong> ${esc(issue.message)}</div>`,
      )
      .join("")}</div>`;
  });
}

/**
 * Canonical source identity resolver.
 * Resolves legacy names and aliases against the catalog while preserving the
 * stable key, physical file name, and display alias as separate values.
 */
function atlasResolveSourceIdentity(sourceLike = {}, catalog = null) {
  const input =
    typeof sourceLike === "string" ? { name: sourceLike } : sourceLike || {};
  const clean = (value) => String(value || "").trim();
  const normalize = (value) => clean(value).toLowerCase();
  const catalogSources = Array.isArray(catalog)
    ? catalog
    : typeof repoLibraryState !== "undefined" &&
        Array.isArray(repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles)
      ? repoLibraryState.sourceFiles
      : [];
  const uniqueMatches = (inputValues, sourceValues) => {
    const wanted = new Set(inputValues.map(normalize).filter(Boolean));
    if (!wanted.size) return [];
    return catalogSources.filter((source) =>
      sourceValues(source)
        .map(normalize)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .some((value) => wanted.has(value)),
    );
  };
  const stableValues = (source) => [
    source.sourceKey,
    source.catalogKey,
    source.sourceReferenceId,
    source.id,
  ];
  const fallbackValues = (source) => [
    source.alias,
    source.sourceFile,
    source.file,
    source.name,
  ];
  const inputStableValues = stableValues(input);
  let matches = uniqueMatches(inputStableValues, stableValues);
  if (matches.length !== 1)
    matches = uniqueMatches(inputStableValues, fallbackValues);
  if (matches.length !== 1) {
    matches = uniqueMatches(fallbackValues(input), (source) => [
      ...stableValues(source),
      ...fallbackValues(source),
    ]);
  }
  const match = matches.length === 1 ? matches[0] : {};
  const ambiguous = matches.length > 1;
  const sourceKey = clean(
    match.sourceKey ||
      match.catalogKey ||
      input.sourceKey ||
      input.catalogKey ||
      match.sourceReferenceId ||
      input.sourceReferenceId ||
      match.id ||
      input.id ||
      match.alias ||
      input.alias ||
      match.sourceFile ||
      input.sourceFile ||
      match.file ||
      input.file ||
      input.name,
  );
  const sourceFile = clean(
    match.sourceFile ||
      input.sourceFile ||
      match.file ||
      input.file ||
      match.name ||
      input.name ||
      sourceKey,
  );
  const alias = clean(
    input.alias || match.alias || input.name || match.name || sourceFile,
  );
  return { sourceKey, sourceFile, alias, catalogSource: match, ambiguous };
}
function atlasNormalizeAuditSourceIdentity(audit) {
  if (!audit || typeof audit !== "object") return audit;
  audit.sources = Array.isArray(audit.sources) ? audit.sources : [];
  audit.sources = audit.sources.map((source) => {
    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
    return {
      ...source,
      sourceKey: identity.sourceKey,
      sourceFile: identity.sourceFile,
      alias: identity.alias,
      name:
        source.name ||
        identity.alias ||
        identity.sourceFile ||
        identity.sourceKey,
    };
  });
  const byAnyIdentity = (value) => atlasResolveSourceIdentity(value);
  (Array.isArray(audit.fields) ? audit.fields : []).forEach((field) => {
    const identity = byAnyIdentity(
      field.sourceKey || field.sourceFile || field.sourceAlias || field.source,
    );
    if (identity.sourceKey) field.sourceKey = identity.sourceKey;
    if (identity.sourceFile) field.sourceFile = identity.sourceFile;
    if (identity.alias) field.sourceAlias = identity.alias;
  });
  (Array.isArray(audit.steps) ? audit.steps : []).forEach((step) => {
    const params =
      step.params && typeof step.params === "object"
        ? step.params
        : (step.params = {});
    const identity = byAnyIdentity(
      step.sourceKey ||
        params.source_key ||
        step.source ||
        params.source_file ||
        params.source_alias,
    );
    if (!identity.sourceKey) return;
    step.sourceKey = identity.sourceKey;
    step.sourceFile = identity.sourceFile;
    step.sourceAlias = identity.alias;
    step.source = identity.sourceKey;
    params.source_key = identity.sourceKey;
    params.source_file = identity.sourceFile;
    params.source_alias = identity.alias;
  });
  return audit;
}

let atlasSourceDerivedVersion = 0;
let atlasSourceCatalogCache = {
  version: -1,
  audit: null,
  entries: [],
  byStable: new Map(),
  byFallback: new Map(),
};
function atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState() {
  atlasSourceDerivedVersion += 1;
  atlasSourceCatalogCache.version = -1;
  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
}
function atlasInvalidateSourceBrowserCaches() {
  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState();
}
function atlasSourceLookupAdd(map, value, source) {
  const key = String(value || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
  if (!key) return;
  const matches = map.get(key) || [];
  matches.push(source);
  map.set(key, matches);
}

const SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE = [
  "#fdd0ec",
  "#b3f5ff",
  "#abf5d1",
  "#fffae6",
  "#c0b6f2",
  "#e6fcff",
  "#ffd9c2",
  "#d8f3dc",
  "#dbeafe",
  "#fde68a",
];

function sourceCatalogEntries() {
  const audit = atlasDisplayDocument?.() || state.audit;
  if (
    atlasSourceCatalogCache.version === atlasSourceDerivedVersion &&
    atlasSourceCatalogCache.audit === audit
  )
    return atlasSourceCatalogCache.entries;
  const entries = new Map();
  (repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles || []).forEach((source) => {
    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
    const key = identity.sourceKey;
    if (!key) return;
    entries.set(key, {
      ...source,
      sourceKey: key,
      sourceFile: identity.sourceFile,
      alias: identity.alias,
      fields: Array.isArray(source.fields) ? source.fields : [],
      fieldCount: source.fieldCount || source.fields?.length || 0,
    });
  });
  (audit.sources || []).forEach((source) => {
    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
    const key = identity.sourceKey;
    if (!key || entries.has(key)) return;
    entries.set(key, {
      ...source,
      sourceKey: key,
      sourceFile: identity.sourceFile,
      alias: identity.alias,
      fields: [],
      fieldCount: 0,
    });
  });
  const sorted = [...entries.values()].sort((a, b) =>
    sourceCatalogDisplayName(a).localeCompare(
      sourceCatalogDisplayName(b),
      undefined,
      { sensitivity: "base", numeric: true },
    ),
  );
  const byStable = new Map(),
    byFallback = new Map();
  sorted.forEach((entry) => {
    [
      entry.sourceKey,
      entry.catalogKey,
      entry.sourceReferenceId,
      entry.id,
    ].forEach((value) => atlasSourceLookupAdd(byStable, value, entry));
    [entry.alias, entry.sourceFile, entry.file, entry.name].forEach((value) =>
      atlasSourceLookupAdd(byFallback, value, entry),
    );
  });
  atlasSourceCatalogCache = {
    version: atlasSourceDerivedVersion,
    audit,
    entries: sorted,
    byStable,
    byFallback,
  };
  return sorted;
}

function selectedSourceDetail(sourceKeyOrFile) {
  const value = String(sourceKeyOrFile || "").trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  const normalized = value.toLowerCase();
  sourceCatalogEntries();
  const stableMatches = atlasSourceCatalogCache.byStable.get(normalized) || [];
  if (stableMatches.length === 1) return stableMatches[0];
  if (stableMatches.length > 1) return null;
  const fallbackMatches =
    atlasSourceCatalogCache.byFallback.get(normalized) || [];
  return fallbackMatches.length === 1 ? fallbackMatches[0] : null;
}

function fieldsForSource(sourceKeyOrFile) {
  const match = selectedSourceDetail(sourceKeyOrFile);
  if (match?.fields?.length) return match.fields;
  const sourceKey = match?.sourceKey || String(sourceKeyOrFile || "").trim();
  const sourceFile = match?.sourceFile || String(sourceKeyOrFile || "").trim();
  const audit = atlasDisplayDocument?.() || state.audit;
  return (audit.fields || [])
    .filter(
      (field) =>
        !sourceKeyOrFile ||
        field.sourceKey === sourceKey ||
        (!field.sourceKey && field.sourceFile === sourceFile),
    )
    .map((field, index) => ({
      order: index + 1,
      fieldName: field.rawFieldName || field.name,
      vanityLabel: field.name,
      label: field.name,
      ...field,
    }));
}

function sourceOptionsHtml(selected = "") {
  return [
    '<option value="">Select source file...</option>',
    ...sourceCatalogEntries().map((source) => {
      const key = source.sourceKey;
      return `<option value="${esc(key)}" ${selected === key || selected === source.sourceFile ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(sourceCatalogDisplayName(source))}</option>`;
    }),
  ].join("");
}

function fieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile = "", selected = "") {
  const fields = fieldsForSource(sourceFile);
  return [
    '<option value="">Select field...</option>',
    ...fields.map((field) => {
      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, sourceFile);
      const label = parts.alternate
        ? `${parts.display} (${parts.alternate})`
        : parts.display;
      return `<option value="${esc(parts.raw)}" ${selected === parts.raw || selected === parts.vanity ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(label)}</option>`;
    }),
  ].join("");
}

function nextSourceColor() {
  const used = new Set(
    (state.audit.sources || []).map((source) => source.color).filter(Boolean),
  );
  return (
    SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.find((color) => !used.has(color)) ||
    SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[
      (state.audit.sources || []).length % SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.length
    ]
  );
}

function atlasSourceMutationKey(source = {}) {
  if (typeof atlasSourceCanonicalKey === "function")
    return atlasSourceCanonicalKey(source);
  if (typeof sourceCatalogKey === "function") return sourceCatalogKey(source);
  return String(
    source.sourceKey || source.name || source.sourceFile || "",
  ).trim();
}

function atlasAddSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile) {
  const source = selectedSourceDetail(sourceKeyOrFile);
  if (!source) {
    status(
      `Source file ${sourceKeyOrFile} was not found in the source library.`,
      "warn",
    );
    return {
      changed: false,
      reason: "not-found",
      sourceKey: String(sourceKeyOrFile || ""),
    };
  }

  const key = atlasSourceMutationKey(source);
  const sourceFile = source.sourceFile || key;
  state.audit.sources = state.audit.sources || [];

  if (
    state.audit.sources.some((item) => atlasSourceMutationKey(item) === key)
  ) {
    return {
      changed: false,
      reason: "already-selected",
      sourceKey: key,
      sourceFile,
    };
  }

  state.audit.sources.push({
    id: uid("source"),
    sourceKey: key,
    name: key,
    sourceFile,
    alias: source.alias || sourceFile,
    color: nextSourceColor(),
    description:
      source.alias && source.alias !== sourceFile
        ? `Alias: ${source.alias}`
        : "",
    sourceMetadata: source.sourceMetadata || {},
  });

  state.audit.fields = (state.audit.fields || []).filter((field) => {
    const fieldKey = String(field.sourceKey || "").trim();
    return fieldKey ? fieldKey !== key : field.sourceFile !== sourceFile;
  });

  (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
    const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label;
    const label = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
    if (!raw) return;
    state.audit.fields.push({
      id: uid("field"),
      name: label,
      type: "Source",
      sourceKey: key,
      sourceFile,
      sourceAlias: source.alias || sourceFile,
      rawFieldName: raw,
      description: `${sourceFile} / ${raw}`,
    });
  });

  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState();
  return { changed: true, action: "add", sourceKey: key, sourceFile };
}

function atlasRemoveSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile) {
  const selected =
    typeof atlasSelectedSourceByKey === "function"
      ? atlasSelectedSourceByKey(sourceKeyOrFile)
      : (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) =>
          [
            source.sourceKey,
            source.name,
            source.sourceFile,
            source.alias,
          ].includes(sourceKeyOrFile),
        );

  if (!selected) {
    status(`Source ${sourceKeyOrFile} is not selected for this audit.`, "warn");
    return {
      changed: false,
      reason: "not-selected",
      sourceKey: String(sourceKeyOrFile || ""),
    };
  }

  const key = atlasSourceMutationKey(selected);
  const sourceFile = selected.sourceFile || selected.name || key;
  const generatedStepIds = new Set(
    (state.audit.steps || [])
      .filter(
        (step) =>
          step.autoGeneratedReference &&
          atlasSourceMutationKey({
            sourceKey: step.source,
            sourceFile: step.source,
          }) === key,
      )
      .map((step) => step.id),
  );

  state.audit.sources = (state.audit.sources || []).filter(
    (source) => atlasSourceMutationKey(source) !== key,
  );
  state.audit.fields = (state.audit.fields || []).filter((field) => {
    const fieldKey = String(field.sourceKey || "").trim();
    return fieldKey ? fieldKey !== key : field.sourceFile !== sourceFile;
  });
  state.audit.steps = (state.audit.steps || []).filter(
    (step) => !generatedStepIds.has(step.id),
  );

  if (state.selectedStepId && generatedStepIds.has(state.selectedStepId)) {
    state.selectedStepId = null;
  }

  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState();
  return {
    changed: true,
    action: "remove",
    sourceKey: key,
    sourceFile,
    selectedStepRemoved: generatedStepIds.size > 0 && !state.selectedStepId,
  };
}

function atlasCommitSourceMutationFallback(action, sourceKeyOrFile) {
  const previousSelectedStepId = state.selectedStepId;
  const result =
    action === "remove"
      ? atlasRemoveSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile)
      : atlasAddSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile);
  if (!result.changed) return result;

  syncAutoReferenceSteps();
  if (
    state.selectedStepId &&
    !state.audit.steps.some((step) => step.id === state.selectedStepId)
  ) {
    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
  }
  if (!state.selectedStepId && previousSelectedStepId) {
    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
  }

  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
  scheduleAutosave();
  status(
    `${action === "remove" ? "Removed" : "Added"} ${result.sourceFile || sourceKeyOrFile} ${action === "remove" ? "from" : "to"} this audit.`,
    "ok",
  );
  return result;
}

function addSourceFileToAudit(sourceKeyOrFile) {
  if (typeof atlasCommitAuditSourceChange === "function") {
    return atlasCommitAuditSourceChange({ action: "add", sourceKeyOrFile });
  }
  return atlasCommitSourceMutationFallback("add", sourceKeyOrFile);
}

function removeSourceFileFromAudit(sourceKeyOrFile) {
  if (typeof atlasCommitAuditSourceChange === "function") {
    return atlasCommitAuditSourceChange({ action: "remove", sourceKeyOrFile });
  }
  return atlasCommitSourceMutationFallback("remove", sourceKeyOrFile);
}

function referenceStepForSource(source, index) {
  const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
  const sourceFile =
    identity.sourceFile || source.sourceFile || source.name || "";
  const sourceAlias = identity.alias || source.alias || sourceFile;
  return {
    id: `reference_${source.id || source.name}`,
    enabled: true,
    autoGeneratedReference: true,
    locked: true,
    parentId: null,
    branchType: "root",
    sortOrder: index + 1,
    title: `Reference ${source.name}`,
    type: "source",
    step_type: "reference_source",
    source: source.name,
    field: "",
    action: "",
    notes:
      "Generated automatically from the source files selected in Sources & Fields.",
    conditions: [],
    params: {
      source_file: sourceFile,
      source_alias: sourceAlias,
    },
  };
}

function syncAutoReferenceSteps() {
  const selected = state.audit.sources || [];
  const manualSteps = (state.audit.steps || []).filter(
    (step) => !step.autoGeneratedReference,
  );
  const referenceSteps = selected.map((source, index) => {
    const existing = (state.audit.steps || []).find(
      (step) => step.autoGeneratedReference && step.source === source.name,
    );
    return {
      ...(existing || referenceStepForSource(source, index)),
      ...referenceStepForSource(source, index),
      id: existing?.id || referenceStepForSource(source, index).id,
    };
  });
  referenceSteps.forEach((step, index) => {
    step.sortOrder = index + 1;
  });
  manualSteps.forEach((step, index) => {
    step.sortOrder = selected.length + index + 1;
  });
  state.audit.steps = [...referenceSteps, ...manualSteps];
}

function applyLibraryFieldToSelectedStep(sourceFile, fieldName) {
  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
    (item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId,
  );
  if (!step)
    return status("Select a step before applying a source field.", "warn");
  if (step.autoGeneratedReference)
    return status(
      "Generated reference steps are controlled by selected sources. Select or add a logic step to apply a field.",
      "warn",
    );
  const sourceResult = atlasAddSourceState(sourceFile);
  if (sourceResult.changed) syncAutoReferenceSteps();
  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
  step.source = sourceFile;
  step.field = fieldName;
  step.params.source_file = sourceFile;
  step.params.source_alias = sourceAliasFor?.(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
  step.params.field_name = fieldName;
  const conditions = parseStepConditions?.(step) || [];
  if (step.step_type === "if_then" && conditions.length) {
    conditions[0].sourceFile = sourceFile;
    conditions[0].sourceAlias = step.params.source_alias;
    conditions[0].fieldName = fieldName;
    step.params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify(conditions);
  }
  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
  showSection("steps");
  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
  scheduleAutosave();
  status(`Applied ${sourceFile}.${fieldName} to the selected step.`, "ok");
}

function allFieldOptions() {
  const options = [];
  (state.audit.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
    if (!field.name) return;
    options.push({
      value: field.name,
      label: `${field.name} (${field.type || "Field"})`,
      kind: field.type || "Field",
      source: field.sourceFile || "Selected Audit Fields",
      raw: field.rawFieldName || field.name,
    });
    if (field.rawFieldName)
      options.push({
        value: field.rawFieldName,
        label: `${field.rawFieldName} (${field.sourceFile || "Raw Field"})`,
        kind: "Raw Field",
        source: field.sourceFile || "Selected Audit Fields",
        raw: field.rawFieldName,
      });
  });
  sourceCatalogEntries().forEach((source) => {
    options.push({
      value: source.sourceKey,
      label: `${sourceCatalogDisplayName(source)} (Source)`,
      kind: "Source",
      source: source.sourceKey,
    });
    (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, source.sourceFile);
      if (parts.raw)
        options.push({
          value: parts.raw,
          label: `${parts.display} (${source.sourceFile}.${parts.raw})`,
          kind: "Source Field",
          source: source.sourceFile,
          raw: parts.raw,
        });
    });
  });
  workbookFieldOptions?.().forEach((field) => options.push(field));
  const seen = new Set();
  return options
    .filter((option) => {
      const key = `${option.value}::${option.label}`;
      if (!option.value || seen.has(key)) return false;
      seen.add(key);
      return true;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
}

function refreshFieldPickerOptions() {
  return atlasMeasure?.("atlas.field-picker.build", () => {
    let fieldList = $("fieldPickerOptions");
    let sourceList = $("sourcePickerOptions");
    if (!fieldList) {
      fieldList = document.createElement("datalist");
      fieldList.id = "fieldPickerOptions";
      document.body.append(fieldList);
    }
    if (!sourceList) {
      sourceList = document.createElement("datalist");
      sourceList.id = "sourcePickerOptions";
      document.body.append(sourceList);
    }
    fieldList.innerHTML = allFieldOptions()
      .map(
        (option) =>
          `<option value="${esc(option.value)}" label="${esc(option.label)}"></option>`,
      )
      .join("");
    sourceList.innerHTML = sourceCatalogEntries()
      .map(
        (source) =>
          `<option value="${esc(source.sourceKey)}" label="${esc(sourceCatalogDisplayName(source))}"></option>`,
      )
      .join("");
    atlasFieldPickerState.initialized = true;
    atlasFieldPickerState.dirty = false;
  });
}

function applyFieldPickerAttributes() {
  document
    .querySelectorAll("[data-step-field], [data-cond-field]")
    .forEach((input) => input.setAttribute("list", "fieldPickerOptions"));
  document
    .querySelectorAll("[data-step-source]")
    .forEach((input) => input.setAttribute("list", "sourcePickerOptions"));
}

function renderFieldPickerPanel() {
  const panel = $("fieldPickerSummary");
  if (!panel) return;
  const sources = sourceCatalogEntries();
  const fieldCount = sources.reduce(
    (total, source) => total + (source.fields?.length || 0),
    0,
  );
  const selectedCount = (state.audit.sources || []).length;
  panel.innerHTML = sources.length
    ? `<p class="hint">${sources.length} source file(s) and ${fieldCount} field(s) available from the local JSON source library. ${selectedCount} source file(s) selected for this audit.</p>`
    : '<p class="hint">No source fields found yet. Confirm audit-index.json points to catalogs/source-file-index.json and run through http://localhost or GitHub Pages.</p>';
}

const atlasFieldPickerState = { initialized: false, dirty: true };

function atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty() {
  atlasFieldPickerState.dirty = true;
}

function atlasEnsureFieldPickerOptions() {
  if (!atlasFieldPickerState.initialized || atlasFieldPickerState.dirty) {
    refreshFieldPickerOptions();
  }
}

// Build backing datalists only when a field/source input is first used.
document.addEventListener(
  "focusin",
  (event) => {
    if (
      !event.target.matches?.(
        "[data-step-field], [data-cond-field], [data-step-source]",
      )
    )
      return;
    atlasEnsureFieldPickerOptions();
    applyFieldPickerAttributes();
  },
  true,
);


================================================================================
END FILE: js/10-field-picker.js
================================================================================

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: js/14-catalog-driven-builder.js
