import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import vm from "node:vm";

function functionSource(path, name) {
  const source = readFileSync(new URL(path, import.meta.url), "utf8");
  const start = source.indexOf(`function ${name}(`);
  assert.notEqual(start, -1, `${name} not found`);
  const brace = source.indexOf("{", start);
  let depth = 0;
  for (let index = brace; index < source.length; index += 1) {
    if (source[index] === "{") depth += 1;
    if (source[index] === "}") depth -= 1;
    if (depth === 0) return source.slice(start, index + 1);
  }
  throw new Error(`${name} body is incomplete`);
}

function loadFunction(path, name, context = {}) {
  const sandbox = vm.createContext(context);
  vm.runInContext(
    `${functionSource(path, name)};this.result=${name};`,
    sandbox,
  );
  return sandbox.result;
}

test("new IF action uses the unique eligible Error Message custom field", () => {
  const fn = loadFunction(
    "../js/15g-visual-ui-events.js",
    "atlasInitializeNewIfThenAction",
    {
      state: { audit: { fields: [] } },
      atlasIsCustomField: (field) => !!field.custom,
    },
  );
  const step = { step_type: "if_then", params: {} };
  fn(step, [{ custom: true, rawFieldName: " Error Message " }]);
  assert.equal(step.params.then_action_type, "display_text");
  assert.equal(step.params.then_output_column, "Error Message");
});

test("new IF action falls back safely for zero, duplicate, or source-only matches", () => {
  const fn = loadFunction(
    "../js/15g-visual-ui-events.js",
    "atlasInitializeNewIfThenAction",
    {
      state: { audit: { fields: [] } },
      atlasIsCustomField: (field) => !!field.custom,
    },
  );
  for (const fields of [
    [],
    [{ custom: false, name: "Error Message" }],
    [
      { custom: true, name: "Error Message" },
      { custom: true, rawFieldName: "error message" },
    ],
  ]) {
    const step = { step_type: "if_then", params: {} };
    fn(step, fields);
    assert.equal(step.params.then_action_type, undefined);
    assert.equal(step.params.then_output_column, undefined);
  }
});

test("new IF action preserves explicitly stored values", () => {
  const fn = loadFunction(
    "../js/15g-visual-ui-events.js",
    "atlasInitializeNewIfThenAction",
    {
      state: { audit: { fields: [] } },
      atlasIsCustomField: () => true,
    },
  );
  const step = {
    step_type: "if_then",
    params: {
      then_action_type: "do_not_display",
      then_output_column: "Review Notes",
    },
  };
  fn(step, [{ custom: true, name: "Error Message" }]);
  assert.deepEqual(step.params, {
    then_action_type: "do_not_display",
    then_output_column: "Review Notes",
  });
});

test("Create Column normalization keeps stored legacy names but leaves a new name blank", () => {
  const fn = loadFunction(
    "../js/14-catalog-driven-builder.js",
    "normalizeCatalogStep",
    {
      LEGACY_TYPE_MAP: {},
      STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS: [["create_column", "Create Column"]],
      legacyTypeFromStepType: () => "create",
      state: {
        audit: { sources: [], fields: [{ name: "First Source Field" }] },
      },
      sourceAliasFor: () => "",
      uid: () => "step_test",
    },
  );
  const fresh = { step_type: "create_column", params: {}, field: "" };
  fn(fresh);
  assert.equal(fresh.params.column_name, "");
  const legacy = {
    step_type: "create_column",
    params: {},
    field: "Legacy Name",
  };
  fn(legacy);
  assert.equal(legacy.params.column_name, "Legacy Name");
});

test("generated Reference Source uses physical file and distinct alias", () => {
  const fn = loadFunction(
    "../js/10-field-picker.js",
    "referenceStepForSource",
    {
      atlasResolveSourceIdentity: () => ({
        sourceFile: "HI0256_Maseke",
        alias: "sap.hi0256",
      }),
    },
  );
  const step = fn(
    { id: "source_2", name: "stable-key", alias: "sap.hi0256" },
    0,
  );
  assert.equal(step.params.source_file, "HI0256_Maseke");
  assert.equal(step.params.source_alias, "sap.hi0256");
});

test("Reviewer Mode native renderers read the detached display document", () => {
  const hierarchy = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/02-hierarchy.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const fields = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/10-field-picker.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const sources = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/15b-source-library-ui.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const selectedSources = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/15e-logic-step-editor.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const virtualLogic = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/15g-visual-ui-events.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const shell = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/16-page-shell.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const displayFields = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/21-display-fields.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );

  assert.match(
    hierarchy,
    /steps = \(atlasDisplayDocument\?\.\(\) \|\| state\.audit\)\.steps \|\| \[\]/,
  );
  assert.match(
    fields,
    /const audit = atlasDisplayDocument\?\.\(\) \|\| state\.audit/,
  );
  assert.match(
    sources,
    /function renderCombinedSelectedSources\(\)[\s\S]*?const audit = atlasDisplayDocument\?\.\(\) \|\| state\.audit/,
  );
  assert.match(
    selectedSources,
    /function atlasSelectedAuditSources\(\)[\s\S]*?const audit = atlasDisplayDocument\?\.\(\) \|\| state\.audit/,
  );
  assert.match(
    virtualLogic,
    /function atlasRenderVirtualLogicRules\(options = \{\}\)[\s\S]*?if \(audit === state\.audit\)/,
  );
  assert.match(
    shell,
    /function updateDashboardMetrics\(\)[\s\S]*?const audit = atlasDisplayDocument\?\.\(\) \|\| state\.audit/,
  );
  assert.match(
    shell,
    /function renderAssignmentEditor\(\)[\s\S]*?normalizeAuditAssignments\?\.\(audit\.assignments\)/,
  );
  assert.match(displayFields, /function renderDisplayFieldsPage\(\)/);
});

test("detached rendering does not redirect editable mutation paths", () => {
  const fieldPicker = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/10-field-picker.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const displayFields = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/21-display-fields.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  assert.match(fieldPicker, /state\.audit\.sources\.push/);
  assert.match(fieldPicker, /state\.audit\.steps/);
  assert.match(displayFields, /atlasDisplayPreferences\(state\.audit\)/);
  assert.match(displayFields, /if \(atlasIsReviewerMode\?\.\(\)\) return/);
});

test("string character count accepts positive whole numbers only", () => {
  const fn = loadFunction(
    "../js/14-catalog-driven-builder.js",
    "atlasPositiveWholeNumber",
  );
  assert.equal(fn("1"), "1");
  assert.equal(fn("005"), "5");
  for (const value of ["", "   ", "0", "-1", "1.5", "cat", "1e2", "Infinity"])
    assert.equal(
      fn(value),
      "",
      `expected ${JSON.stringify(value)} to be invalid`,
    );
});

test("string character count authoring uses guarded numeric controls", () => {
  const builder = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/14-catalog-driven-builder.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const events = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/15g-visual-ui-events.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  assert.match(builder, /type="number"[\s\S]*?min="1"[\s\S]*?step="1"/);
  assert.match(builder, /paramName === "string_character_count"/);
  assert.match(builder, /paramName === "then_string_character_count"/);
  assert.match(events, /atlasCommitLiveEditorWithStringCharacterCountGuard/);
  assert.match(events, /atlasValidateStringCharacterCountInput/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(events, /!event\.target\.value \|\|/);
});

test("invalid string character counts use a safe Analyst Logic placeholder", () => {
  const source = readFileSync(
    new URL("../js/07-phrase-catalog.js", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  assert.match(
    source,
    /atlasPositiveWholeNumber\(config\.count\) \|\| "\[positive whole number\]"/,
  );
  assert.doesNotMatch(
    source,
    /atlasPositiveWholeNumber\(config\.count\) \|\| config\.count/,
  );
});

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: test/render-scheduling.test.js
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import fs from "node:fs";
import test from "node:test";
import vm from "node:vm";

function functionSource(source, name) {
  const start = source.indexOf(`function ${name}(`);
  assert.notEqual(start, -1, `${name} must exist`);
  const brace = source.indexOf(") {", start) + 2;
  let depth = 0;
  for (let index = brace; index < source.length; index += 1) {
    if (source[index] === "{") depth += 1;
    if (source[index] === "}") depth -= 1;
    if (depth === 0) return source.slice(start, index + 1);
  }
  throw new Error(`Could not extract ${name}`);
}

const source = fs.readFileSync(
  new URL("../js/02-logic-rendering.js", import.meta.url),
  "utf8",
);
const context = {};
vm.runInNewContext(
  `${functionSource(source, "atlasMergeRenderFlags")}; this.merge = atlasMergeRenderFlags;`,
  context,
);

test("render flags merge without canceling pending work", () => {
  const merged = context.merge(
    { preview: true, selectedStep: true },
    { preview: false, validation: true },
  );
  assert.equal(merged.preview, true);
  assert.equal(merged.selectedStep, true);
  assert.equal(merged.validation, true);
});

test("steps subsume a separately requested selected-step render", () => {
  const merged = context.merge(
    { selectedStep: true, logicTable: true },
    { steps: true },
  );
  assert.equal(merged.steps, true);
  assert.equal(merged.selectedStep, false);
  assert.equal(merged.logicTable, true);
});

test("immediate requests remain sticky within a batch", () => {
  const merged = context.merge(
    { immediate: true },
    { immediate: false, preview: true },
  );
  assert.equal(merged.immediate, true);
  assert.equal(merged.preview, true);
});

END FILE: test/render-scheduling.test.js

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: test/atlas-audit-issues.test.js
================================================================================

import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import vm from "node:vm";

const engineSource = readFileSync(
  new URL("../js/22-audit-issue-engine.js", import.meta.url),
  "utf8",
);
const adapterSource = readFileSync(
  new URL("../js/23-atlas-audit-issues.js", import.meta.url),
  "utf8",
);
const catalog = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(
    new URL("../catalogs/audit-issue-rules.json", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  ),
);

async function runtime(audit, extras = {}) {
  const sandbox = {
    console,
    __auditInput: audit,
    __auditSelected: extras.auditSelected ?? true,
    repoLibraryState: { sourceFiles: extras.sourceFiles || [] },
    workbookState: { warnings: extras.workbookWarnings || [] },
    parseStepConditions(step) {
      if (step.params?.conditions_json)
        return JSON.parse(step.params.conditions_json);
      return step.conditions || [];
    },
    atlasValidateMathOperations:
      extras.atlasValidateMathOperations || (() => []),
    atlasValidateStringTransform:
      extras.atlasValidateStringTransform || (() => []),
    ATLAS_STRING_TRANSFORM_VALUE_MODE: "String transformation",
    fetch: async () => ({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => structuredClone(catalog),
    }),
    renderValidationPanel() {},
  };
  if (extras.atlasResolveSourceIdentity)
    sandbox.atlasResolveSourceIdentity = extras.atlasResolveSourceIdentity;
  sandbox.window = sandbox;
  const context = vm.createContext(sandbox);
  vm.runInContext(
    `const state = { audit: __auditInput, auditSelected: __auditSelected };
     function hasSelectedAudit() { return state.auditSelected === true; }`,
    context,
  );
  vm.runInContext(engineSource, context);
  vm.runInContext(adapterSource, context);
  await context.ATLASAuditIssues.catalogReady;
  return context;
}

const completeAudit = () => ({
  name: "Complete audit",
  purpose: "Test purpose",
  assignments: {
    assigned: [{ id: "a" }],
    backup: [{ id: "b" }],
    developer: [{ id: "d" }],
  },
  sources: [{ sourceKey: "source.one", sourceFile: "Source One" }],
  fields: [{ sourceKey: "source.one", rawFieldName: "Status", name: "Status" }],
  displayFields: [{ sourceKey: "source.one", rawFieldName: "Status" }],
  steps: [
    {
      id: "step-1",
      enabled: true,
      parentId: null,
      branchType: "root",
      step_type: "if_then",
      params: {
        then_action_type: "do_not_display",
        conditions_json: JSON.stringify([
          {
            id: "condition-1",
            fieldName: "Status",
            operator: "is equal to",
            valueMode: "values",
            compareValue: "Inactive",
          },
        ]),
      },
    },
  ],
});

test("buildContext does not mutate the supplied audit", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  const before = structuredClone(audit);
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  context.ATLASAuditIssues.buildContext(audit);
  assert.deepEqual(audit, before);
});

test("complete audit produces one canonical result without setup or logic issues", async () => {
  const context = await runtime(completeAudit());
  const result = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current();
  assert.equal(result.status, "ok");
  assert.equal(result.issues.length, 0);
  assert.deepEqual([...context.atlasLogicIssues()], []);
  assert.deepEqual([...context.ATLASAuditIssues.legacyIssues()], []);
});

test("missing setup values use requested warning severities", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.name = "";
  audit.purpose = "";
  audit.assignments = { assigned: [], backup: [], developer: [] };
  audit.sources = [];
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  const result = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current();
  const rules = new Set(result.issues.map((entry) => entry.ruleId));
  assert.ok(rules.has("audit.identity.name-missing"));
  assert.ok(rules.has("audit.assignments.assigned-missing"));
  assert.ok(rules.has("audit.sources.none"));
  assert.ok(result.issues.every((entry) => entry.severity === "warning"));
});

test("missing condition field and operator are critical", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps[0].params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify([
    {
      id: "condition-1",
      fieldName: "",
      operator: "",
      valueMode: "field",
      compareSourceFile: "",
      compareFieldName: "",
    },
  ]);
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  const result = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current();
  const conditionIssues = result.issues.filter(
    (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.condition-integrity",
  );
  assert.ok(conditionIssues.length >= 4);
  assert.ok(conditionIssues.every((entry) => entry.severity === "critical"));
  assert.equal(result.status, "critical");
});

test("valid descendant action suppresses parent missing-action warning", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps[0].params.then_action_type = "freeform";
  audit.steps[0].params.then_action = "";
  audit.steps.push({
    id: "step-2",
    enabled: true,
    parentId: "step-1",
    branchType: "then",
    step_type: "if_then",
    params: {
      then_action_type: "do_not_display",
      conditions_json: JSON.stringify([
        {
          id: "c2",
          fieldName: "Status",
          operator: "is equal to",
          valueMode: "values",
          compareValue: "X",
        },
      ]),
    },
  });
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  const issues = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues;
  assert.equal(
    issues.some(
      (entry) =>
        entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.action-missing" &&
        entry.target.stepId === "step-1",
    ),
    false,
  );
});

test("disabled descendant does not suppress parent missing-action warning", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps[0].params.then_action_type = "freeform";
  audit.steps[0].params.then_action = "";
  audit.steps.push({
    id: "step-2",
    enabled: false,
    parentId: "step-1",
    branchType: "then",
    step_type: "if_then",
    params: { then_action_type: "do_not_display", conditions_json: "[]" },
  });
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  const issues = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues;
  assert.equal(
    issues.some(
      (entry) =>
        entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.action-missing" &&
        entry.target.stepId === "step-1",
    ),
    true,
  );
});

test("field relationship rule validates direct, indirect, reverse, same-source, alternate, cyclic, and duplicate paths", async () => {
  const sourceFiles = ["A", "B", "C", "D"].map((sourceKey) => ({
    sourceKey,
    sourceFile: `${sourceKey}.csv`,
    alias: `Source ${sourceKey}`,
    fields: [{ rawFieldName: "id" }, { rawFieldName: "value" }],
  }));
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.sources = sourceFiles.map(({ sourceKey, sourceFile, alias }) => ({
    sourceKey,
    sourceFile,
    alias,
  }));
  audit.fields = sourceFiles.flatMap(({ sourceKey, sourceFile }) =>
    ["id", "value"].map((rawFieldName) => ({
      sourceKey,
      sourceFile,
      rawFieldName,
      name: rawFieldName,
    })),
  );
  audit.displayFields = [{ sourceKey: "A", rawFieldName: "value" }];
  const joins = [
    ["A", "B"],
    ["B", "C"],
    ["A", "D"],
    ["C", "A"],
    ["A", "B"],
  ];
  audit.steps = [
    ...joins.map(([left, right], index) => ({
      id: `join-${index}`,
      enabled: true,
      step_type: "join",
      params: {
        join_type: "INNER JOIN",
        left_source: left,
        right_source: right,
        left_field: "id",
        right_field: "id",
      },
    })),
    {
      id: "compare",
      enabled: true,
      step_type: "if_then",
      params: {
        then_action_type: "do_not_display",
        conditions_json: JSON.stringify(
          [
            ["A", "B"],
            ["A", "C"],
            ["C", "A"],
            ["A", "A"],
            ["C", "D"],
          ].map(([left, right], index) => ({
            id: `condition-${index}`,
            sourceFile: left,
            fieldName: "value",
            operator: "is equal to",
            valueMode: "field",
            compareSourceFile: right,
            compareFieldName: "value",
          })),
        ),
      },
    },
  ];
  const result = (
    await runtime(audit, { sourceFiles })
  ).ATLASAuditIssues.current();
  assert.equal(
    result.issues.filter(
      (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.field-relationship-invalid",
    ).length,
    0,
  );
});
test("field relationship rule reports disconnected, disabled, incomplete, and UNION paths", async () => {
  for (const join of [
    null,
    {
      enabled: false,
      join_type: "INNER JOIN",
      left_field: "id",
      right_field: "id",
    },
    {
      enabled: true,
      join_type: "INNER JOIN",
      left_field: "",
      right_field: "id",
    },
    { enabled: true, join_type: "UNION", left_field: "", right_field: "" },
  ]) {
    const audit = completeAudit();
    audit.sources = ["A", "B"].map((sourceKey) => ({ sourceKey }));
    audit.fields = ["A", "B"].flatMap((sourceKey) =>
      ["id", "value"].map((rawFieldName) => ({
        sourceKey,
        rawFieldName,
        name: rawFieldName,
      })),
    );
    audit.displayFields = [{ sourceKey: "A", rawFieldName: "value" }];
    audit.steps = [
      ...(join
        ? [
            {
              id: "join",
              enabled: join.enabled,
              step_type: "join",
              params: {
                join_type: join.join_type,
                left_source: "A",
                right_source: "B",
                left_field: join.left_field,
                right_field: join.right_field,
              },
            },
          ]
        : []),
      {
        id: "compare",
        enabled: true,
        step_type: "if_then",
        params: {
          then_action_type: "do_not_display",
          conditions_json: JSON.stringify([
            {
              id: "condition-1",
              sourceFile: "A",
              fieldName: "value",
              operator: "is equal to",
              valueMode: "field",
              compareSourceFile: "B",
              compareFieldName: "value",
            },
          ]),
        },
      },
    ];
    const issues = (
      await runtime(audit)
    ).ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
      (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.field-relationship-invalid",
    );
    assert.equal(issues.length, 1);
    assert.equal(issues[0].severity, "critical");
    assert.equal(issues[0].target.stepId, "compare");
    assert.equal(issues[0].target.conditionId, "condition-1");
  }
});
test("field relationship rule resolves unique aliases and ignores ambiguous identities", async () => {
  const sourceFiles = [
    { sourceKey: "A", sourceFile: "A.csv", alias: "Alpha" },
    { sourceKey: "B", sourceFile: "B.csv", alias: "Beta" },
  ].map((source) => ({
    ...source,
    fields: [{ rawFieldName: "id" }, { rawFieldName: "value" }],
  }));
  const resolver = (sourceLike, catalogSources) => {
    const raw = String(
      typeof sourceLike === "string"
        ? sourceLike
        : sourceLike.sourceKey ||
            sourceLike.sourceFile ||
            sourceLike.alias ||
            "",
    ).trim();
    const matches = catalogSources.filter((source) =>
      [source.sourceKey, source.sourceFile, source.alias].includes(raw),
    );
    return matches.length === 1
      ? { ...matches[0], ambiguous: false }
      : { sourceKey: "", sourceFile: "", ambiguous: matches.length > 1 };
  };
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.sources = sourceFiles;
  audit.fields = sourceFiles.flatMap(({ sourceKey, sourceFile }) =>
    ["id", "value"].map((rawFieldName) => ({
      sourceKey,
      sourceFile,
      rawFieldName,
      name: rawFieldName,
    })),
  );
  audit.displayFields = [{ sourceKey: "A", rawFieldName: "value" }];
  audit.steps = [
    {
      id: "join",
      enabled: true,
      step_type: "join",
      params: {
        join_type: "LEFT JOIN",
        left_source: "Alpha",
        right_source: "Beta",
        left_field: "id",
        right_field: "id",
      },
    },
    {
      id: "compare",
      enabled: true,
      step_type: "if_then",
      params: {
        then_action_type: "do_not_display",
        conditions_json: JSON.stringify([
          {
            id: "condition-1",
            sourceFile: "A",
            fieldName: "value",
            operator: "is equal to",
            valueMode: "field",
            compareSourceFile: "B",
            compareFieldName: "value",
          },
          {
            id: "condition-2",
            sourceFile: "Unknown",
            fieldName: "value",
            operator: "is equal to",
            valueMode: "field",
            compareSourceFile: "B",
            compareFieldName: "value",
          },
        ]),
      },
    },
  ];
  const issues = (
    await runtime(audit, { sourceFiles, atlasResolveSourceIdentity: resolver })
  ).ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
    (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.field-relationship-invalid",
  );
  assert.equal(issues.length, 0);
});
test("field relationship issues are deterministic per condition and evaluation does not mutate the audit", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.sources = ["A", "B", "C"].map((sourceKey) => ({ sourceKey }));
  audit.fields = ["A", "B", "C"].map((sourceKey) => ({
    sourceKey,
    rawFieldName: "value",
    name: "value",
  }));
  audit.displayFields = [{ sourceKey: "A", rawFieldName: "value" }];
  audit.steps = [
    {
      id: "compare",
      enabled: true,
      step_type: "if_then",
      params: {
        then_action_type: "do_not_display",
        conditions_json: JSON.stringify([
          {
            id: "condition-1",
            sourceFile: "A",
            fieldName: "value",
            operator: "is equal to",
            valueMode: "field",
            compareSourceFile: "B",
            compareFieldName: "value",
          },
          {
            id: "condition-2",
            sourceFile: "A",
            fieldName: "value",
            operator: "is equal to",
            valueMode: "field",
            compareSourceFile: "C",
            compareFieldName: "value",
          },
        ]),
      },
    },
  ];
  const before = structuredClone(audit);
  const issues = (
    await runtime(audit)
  ).ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
    (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.field-relationship-invalid",
  );
  assert.equal(issues.length, 2);
  assert.equal(new Set(issues.map((entry) => entry.stableKey)).size, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(audit, before);
});

      "utf8",
    ),
  );

    ...new Set(catalog.rules.map((rule) => rule.fixAction).filter(Boolean)),

    "addAssignment",

      1,

});

function stringTransformValidator(value, prefix = "") {
  const operation = value[`${prefix}string_operation`] || "first_characters";
  const count = String(value[`${prefix}string_character_count`] ?? "").trim();
  if (operation !== "character_count" && !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(count))
    return [
      {
        level: "error",
        code: "string_count",
        message: "Number of characters must be a positive whole number.",
      },
    ];
  return [];
}

test("invalid persisted Create Column string counts produce critical Audit Issues", async () => {
  for (const value of ["", "0", "-1", "1.5", "cat", "1e2"]) {
    const audit = completeAudit();
    audit.steps = [
      {
        id: "string-step",
        enabled: true,
        parentId: null,
        branchType: "root",
        step_type: "create_column",
        params: {
          column_name: "Result",
          value_mode: "String transformation",
          string_operation: "first_characters",
          string_source_file: "Source One",
          string_source_field: "Status",
          string_character_count: value,
        },
      },
    ];
    const context = await runtime(audit, {
      atlasValidateStringTransform: stringTransformValidator,
    });
    const issues = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
      (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.string-transform-integrity",
    );
    assert.equal(
      issues.length,
      1,
      `expected issue for ${JSON.stringify(value)}`,
    );
    assert.equal(issues[0].severity, "critical");
    assert.equal(issues[0].target.stepId, "string-step");
  }
});

test("invalid persisted IF action string counts produce critical Audit Issues", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps[0].params.then_action_type = "set_column_string_transform";
  Object.assign(audit.steps[0].params, {
    then_target_column: "Result",
    then_string_operation: "last_characters",
    then_string_source_file: "Source One",
    then_string_source_field: "Status",
    then_string_character_count: "cat",
  });
  const context = await runtime(audit, {
    atlasValidateStringTransform: stringTransformValidator,
  });
  const issues = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
    (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.string-transform-integrity",
  );
  assert.equal(issues.length, 1);
  assert.equal(issues[0].severity, "critical");
});

test("Character Count ignores stale character-count data", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps = [
    {
      id: "character-count-step",
      enabled: true,
      parentId: null,
      branchType: "root",
      step_type: "create_column",
      params: {
        column_name: "Length",
        value_mode: "String transformation",
        string_operation: "character_count",
        string_source_file: "Source One",
        string_source_field: "Status",
        string_character_count: "cat",
      },
    },
  ];
  const context = await runtime(audit, {
    atlasValidateStringTransform: stringTransformValidator,
  });
  assert.equal(
    context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.some(
      (entry) => entry.ruleId === "audit.logic.string-transform-integrity",
    ),
    false,
  );
});

test("incomplete Concatenate fields produce critical Audit Issues", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps = [
    {
      id: "concat-step",
      enabled: true,
      parentId: null,
      branchType: "root",
      step_type: "create_column",
      params: {
        column_name: "Combined",
        value_mode: "Concatenate fields",
        concat_field_2: "Status",
        concat_source_2: "Source One",
      },
    },
  ];
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  const issues = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
    (entry) =>
      entry.target.stepId === "concat-step" &&
      /Concatenate Fields rule/.test(entry.summary),
  );
  assert.equal(issues.length, 1);
  assert.equal(issues[0].severity, "critical");
  assert.match(issues[0].summary, /missing its first field or source/i);
});

test("complete Concatenate fields add no incomplete-rule issue", async () => {
  const audit = completeAudit();
  audit.steps = [
    {
      id: "concat-step",
      enabled: true,
      parentId: null,
      branchType: "root",
      step_type: "create_column",
      params: {
        column_name: "Combined",
        value_mode: "Concatenate fields",
        concat_field_1: "Status",
        concat_source_1: "Source One",
        concat_field_2: "Status",
        concat_source_2: "Source One",
      },
    },
  ];
  const context = await runtime(audit);
  const issues = context.ATLASAuditIssues.current().issues.filter(
    (entry) =>
      entry.target.stepId === "concat-step" &&
      /Concatenate Fields rule/.test(entry.summary),
  );
  assert.equal(issues.length, 0);
});

BEGIN FILE: tests/fixtures/atlas.fixture.js
================================================================================

import { test as base, expect } from "@playwright/test";
import { lifecycleConfig, uniqueAuditName } from "../helpers/config.js";
import {
  acceptNextConfirmation,
  activeAuditSnapshot,
  attachJson,
  waitForAtlasReady,
} from "../helpers/ui.js";
import { isAuditCreateResponse, responseJson } from "../helpers/api.js";

export const test = base.extend({
  atlasConfig: async ({}, use) => use(lifecycleConfig()),
  ownedAudit: async ({ page, atlasConfig }, use, testInfo) => {
    const owned = {
      id: null,
      name: uniqueAuditName(atlasConfig.prefix, testInfo.workerIndex),
      initialRevision: null,
    };
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page).toHaveTitle(/ATLAS Align/i);
    await waitForAtlasReady(page);
    const createButton = page.locator("#newAuditBtn");
    await expect(
      createButton,
      "The authenticated identity must be able to create Preview audits",
    ).toBeVisible();
    await expect(createButton).toBeEnabled();
    await acceptNextConfirmation(page);
    const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse(isAuditCreateResponse);
    await createButton.click();
    const payload = await responseJson(await responsePromise);
    await expect
      .poll(async () => (await activeAuditSnapshot(page))?.revisionId)
      .toBeTruthy();
    const snapshot = await activeAuditSnapshot(page);
    owned.id = payload.audit?.id || payload.id || snapshot?.id || null;
    owned.initialRevision = payload.revision || payload.currentRevision || null;
    expect(
      owned.id,
      "POST /api/audits must return or activate a new audit ID",
    ).toBeTruthy();
    await attachJson(testInfo, "owned-audit.json", {
      name: owned.name,
      auditId: owned.id,
      initialRevision: owned.initialRevision,
    });
    await use(owned);
    const failed = testInfo.status !== testInfo.expectedStatus;
    if (failed && atlasConfig.preserveFailed) return;
    if (!owned.id) return;
    const current = await activeAuditSnapshot(page);
    if (current?.id !== owned.id) return;
    const deleteButton = page.locator("#deleteAuditBtn");
    if (!(await deleteButton.isVisible()) || !(await deleteButton.isEnabled()))
      return;
    await acceptNextConfirmation(page);
    const archive = page.waitForResponse((response) => {
      const url = new URL(response.url());
      return (
        response.request().method() === "DELETE" &&
        url.pathname === `/api/audits/${encodeURIComponent(owned.id)}`
      );
    });
    await deleteButton.click();
    const response = await archive;
    expect(
      response.ok(),
      "Successful tests archive only their exact owned audit",
    ).toBeTruthy();
  },
});

export { expect };

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: tests/helpers/ui.js

import { expect } from "@playwright/test";

export async function waitForAtlasReady(page) {
  await expect(page.locator('body[data-atlas-ready="true"]')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator("#page-my-audits.page.active")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator("#newAuditBtn")).toBeVisible();
}

export async function acceptNextConfirmation(page) {
  page.once("dialog", async (dialog) => {
    expect(dialog.type()).toBe("confirm");
    await dialog.accept();
  });
}

export async function openPage(page, section) {
  await page.evaluate(async (target) => {
    if (typeof window.atlasNavigateToSection === "function") {
      await window.atlasNavigateToSection(target);
      return;
    }

    if (typeof window.showSection === "function") {
      window.showSection(target);
      return;
    }

    throw new Error(`No supported ATLAS navigation function for ${target}`);
  }, section);

  await expect(
    page.locator(`.page.active:is([data-page="${section}"], #page-${section})`),
    `Expected ATLAS page "${section}" to become active`,
  ).toBeVisible();
}

export async function openHistory(page) {
  const openButton = page.getByRole("button", {
    name: "View Audit History",
    exact: true,
  });

  await expect(
    openButton,
    "Expected workflow History capability to become available",
  ).toBeVisible();

  await expect(openButton).toBeEnabled();

  await openButton.click();

  await expect(
    page.locator("#page-history.page.active"),
    "Expected Audit History to become active",
  ).toBeVisible();

  await expect(
    page.locator("#atlasHistoryTimeline [role='listitem']").first(),
    "Expected Audit History to load at least one timeline entry",
  ).toBeVisible();

  await expect(page.locator("#atlasHistoryStatus")).toContainText(/revision/i);
}

export async function activeAuditSnapshot(page) {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const appState = typeof state !== "undefined" ? state : window.state;

    const audit = appState?.audit;
    const persistence = appState?.auditPersistence || {};

    if (!audit) return null;

    return {
      id: audit.id,
      name: audit.name,
      description: audit.description ?? audit.purpose ?? "",
      status: audit.status,
      steps: structuredClone(audit.steps || []),
      sources: structuredClone(audit.sources || audit.selectedSources || []),
      displayFields: structuredClone(audit.displayFields || []),
      persistenceAuditId: persistence.auditId || null,
      revisionId: persistence.revisionId || null,
      revisionNumber: persistence.revisionNumber || null,
    };
  });
}

export async function openOwnedAudit(page, auditId) {
  const id = String(auditId || "").trim();
  expect(id, "A test-owned audit ID is required").toBeTruthy();

  const search = page.getByRole("searchbox", { name: "Search audits" });
  await search.fill(id);

  let openButton = page.locator(`[data-open-database-audit="${id}"]`);

  if ((await openButton.count()) === 0) {
    await page.locator('[data-my-audits-role="all"]').click();
    await search.fill(id);
  }

  openButton = page.locator(`[data-open-database-audit="${id}"]`);
  await expect(
    openButton,
    `Expected My Audits to contain owned audit ${id}`,
  ).toHaveCount(1);

  const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse((response) => {
    const url = new URL(response.url());
    return (
      response.request().method() === "GET" &&
      url.pathname === `/api/audits/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`
    );
  });

  await openButton.click();
  const response = await responsePromise;
  expect(response.ok(), `GET /api/audits/${id} must succeed`).toBeTruthy();

  await expect
    .poll(async () => {
      const snapshot = await activeAuditSnapshot(page);
      return {
        id: snapshot?.id || null,
        persistenceAuditId: snapshot?.persistenceAuditId || null,
        hasRevision: Boolean(snapshot?.revisionId),
      };
    })
    .toEqual({
      id,
      persistenceAuditId: id,
      hasRevision: true,
    });

  await expect(page.locator("#page-overview.page.active")).toBeVisible();
}

export async function setOverview(page, name, description) {
  const nameInput = page.locator("#auditName");
  const purposeInput = page.locator("#auditPurpose");
  await nameInput.fill(name);
  await nameInput.blur();
  await purposeInput.fill(description);
  await purposeInput.blur();
  await expect(nameInput).toHaveValue(name);
  await expect(purposeInput).toHaveValue(description);
}

export async function attachJson(testInfo, name, value) {
  await testInfo.attach(name, {
    body: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)),
    contentType: "application/json",
  });
}

import { test, expect } from "./fixtures/atlas.fixture.js";

  ownedAudit,
}, testInfo) => {

    .locator(

  await attachJson(testInfo, "scenario-5-12-step-types.json", options);

BEGIN FILE: tests/logic-builder-runtime-options.spec.js

import { test, expect } from "./fixtures/atlas.fixture.js";
import { openPage, attachJson } from "./helpers/ui.js";

const groups = [
  ["6-6.12", "Create Column"],
  ["7-7.16", "IF / THEN"],
  ["8-8.4", "ELSE"],
  ["9-9.10", "Join / Union Sources"],
  ["10-10.3", "Format Field"],
  ["11-11.2", "Sort Records"],
];

for (const [scenario, label] of groups) {
  test(`${scenario} discovers and records ${label} runtime controls`, async ({
    page,
    ownedAudit,
  }, testInfo) => {
    await openPage(page, "steps");
    await page.locator("#addStepBtn").click();
    const choice = page
      .getByRole("button", {
        name: new RegExp(label.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&"), "i"),
      })
      .first();
    test.skip(
      !(await choice.isVisible()),
      `${label} is not exposed by the deployed UI`,
    );
    await choice.click();
    const controls = await page
      .locator(
        "#selectedStepDetails input, #selectedStepDetails textarea, #selectedStepDetails select, #selectedStepDetails button",
      )
      .evaluateAll((nodes) =>
        nodes.map((node) => ({
          tag: node.tagName.toLowerCase(),
          id: node.id || null,
          name: node.getAttribute("name"),
          label:
            node.getAttribute("aria-label") ||
            (node.textContent || "").trim() ||
            null,
          type: node.getAttribute("type"),
          options:
            node.tagName === "SELECT"
              ? [...node.options].map((option) => option.textContent.trim())
              : undefined,
        })),
      );
    expect(controls.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    await attachJson(testInfo, `scenario-${scenario}-controls.json`, controls);
  });
}

// @ts-check

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { defineConfig, devices } from "@playwright/test";

const localEnvPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env");
if (fs.existsSync(localEnvPath)) {
  process.loadEnvFile(localEnvPath);
}

const authFile = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "playwright/.auth/studioshare.json",
);

function requirePublicPreviewUrl(value) {
  if (!value?.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      "PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL is required. Set it in .env to the public StudioShare Preview URL.",
    );
  }

  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = new URL(value.trim());
  } catch {
    throw new Error("PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL must be a valid absolute URL.");
  }

  if (parsed.protocol !== "https:") {
    throw new Error("PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL must use HTTPS.");
  }

  return parsed.origin;
}

const baseURL = requirePublicPreviewUrl(process.env.PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL);

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "./tests",
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  forbidOnly: Boolean(process.env.CI),
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
  reporter: [["html", { open: "never" }]],

  use: {
    baseURL,
    trace: "retain-on-failure",
    screenshot: "only-on-failure",
    video: "retain-on-failure",
  },

  projects: [
    {
      name: "setup",
      testMatch: /auth\.setup\.js/,
      use: {
        ...devices["Desktop Chrome"],
        headless: false,
      },
    },
    {
      name: "chromium",
      testIgnore: /auth\.setup\.js/,
      use: {
        ...devices["Desktop Chrome"],
        storageState: authFile,
      },
    },
  ],
});
